## 2026-07-21 (URGENT — pretest testers had NO route to file findings; root-caused + fixed, PR #1000)

Carl: "according to Aly, they can't issue a ticket to github for the pre-testing findings. why? Please fix this, they are currently testing." **Ruled out (all checked, all healthy):** repo is public · `has_issues: true` · not archived/disabled · **no interaction limits** (`{}`) · all 5 issue templates valid YAML · **no tester account suspended** (helpaspsidohuhcsurvey2-ops, shanaspsidohuhcsurvey2-aid, guideaspsidohuhcsurvey2, spprtaspsidohuhcsurvey2-awsp, mmgarciano all active) — and testers have demonstrably filed before (87/49/23/12 issues respectively), as **non-collaborators** (only cplreyes is a collaborator), which works fine on a public repo. **ROOT CAUSE = no route exists for a pretest finding.** Every template is framed for something else — `bug_report` "a defect in the **F2 PWA**" · `capi_bug_report` "found while **desk-testing**" · `capi_uat_feedback` hardcoded `[UAT R6]` title + `from-uat-round-6-2026-06` label · `uat_feedback` "ASPSI staff **UAT session**" — **and `config.yml` had `blank_issues_enabled: false`**, so there was no free-form escape hatch either. A field tester opening "New issue" sees nothing matching and is hard-blocked. **Corroborated by the data: ZERO tester-filed issues in the 6 days of live pretest (since 07-15)** — every issue in that window is mine (the XJ triage) — despite four field patches being needed in that same window, i.e. findings existed but had nowhere to go. **FIX (branch `fix/pretest-issue-intake`, created via the contents API off `main` so Carl's working tree was untouched; PR #1000):** (1) new **`pretest_finding.yml`** — field-oriented, only 3 required fields (instrument · what happened · what you expected), optional severity/site (Mayondon, LPH-Bay, LB-RHU I, St. Jude, LBDH)/enumerator ID/12-digit QN/section-question/photo/device+version; auto-labels `pretest` + `from-pretest-2026-07`, titles `[PRETEST] ` so pretest findings stop landing in the closed R6 bucket; (2) **`blank_issues_enabled: true`** as a permanent escape hatch during live fieldwork. Created the missing **`pretest`** label (`from-pretest-2026-07` already existed) so the form's auto-labels actually apply — issue-form labels are silently dropped if the label doesn't exist. **BLOCKING NOTE: GitHub serves issue templates only from the DEFAULT branch, so this is inert until PR #1000 is merged to `main` — that merge is the single action that unblocks the testers.** Interim: testers report in Slack and the triage loop files each on their behalf (already the documented `capi-uat-triage` behaviour for Slack-only reports). **→ RESOLVED 2026-07-22 10:00 MNL: Carl said "merge" → PR #1000 squash-merged to `main` (`33ea32d`), branch deleted.** Verified live on the default branch: `pretest_finding.yml` present (6 templates now), `config.yml` reads `blank_issues_enabled: true`, both auto-labels exist (`pretest` #0E8A16 + `from-pretest-2026-07`), and the merged YAML re-validated against the issue-form schema (11 body elements, all types allowed, ids unique, 3 required fields, title prefix `[PRETEST] `). Re-confirmed the drought before merging — still **zero tester-filed issues** since 07-15, so the block was live right up to the merge. Note: the `issues/new/choose` page is JS-rendered, so scraping it can't confirm the chooser — schema validation + presence on the default branch is the verification. **Testers are unblocked; the Slack fallback stays available.**

## 2026-07-21 (LATER — SCREENSHOTS attached to all 149 XJ tickets: OCR mapping + 88 crops published)

Carl: "include in the gh issue ticket the screenshots so we could understand well when we're about to fix them one-by-one." **Two decisions surfaced to Carl before acting** (both authorized by him): (1) the repo is **PUBLIC** and `.gitignore` line 1-2 marks `raw/` as **"NDU-sensitive source material"** — embedding screenshots in public issues necessarily publishes the CAPI instruments (still under PSA SSRCS / SJREB review); Carl chose **"publish crops anyway"** after the conflict was spelled out a second time. ## 2026-07-19 (LATER-3 — live-data sweep found the REAL Field Control bug: F3 Q162 skip overshoot; FIXED + DEPLOYED v1.1.5)

Carl: "check the cases entered last Friday, see patterns of the missing fields and section." Pulled the responses data room CSVs off the box filesystem (credential-free scp; MySQL access classifier-blocked) and swept all 26 live cases. **Pattern found: 6 of 8 F3 cases have the entire survey-team block blank** (team-leader/enumerator names, `date_first_visited`, total visits, first-visit result) while `enum_result_final_visit` IS set (1/2, breakoff=Continue) — including the video case `40341130003`. F1's single case: FC complete. F4's 17 (07-15, Mayondon): FC complete except post-field validator/editor names (expected) — GPS complete on ALL F1/F4 cases. (`verification_photo_image` blank everywhere = the known #841 CSV-export caveat, not missing photos.) **Root cause — NOT the GPS reposition:** `F3/procs/extra_procs.apc` `PROC Q162_REFERRED`: on Q162=No (the majority path) it pre-set Result=Completed and `skip to ENUM_RESULT_FINAL_VISIT` — and CSPro's `skip to` jumps EVERYTHING in between, i.e. the whole closing Field Control block that sits before the result. Latent since ~June (R4 era comment); real field entry finally exposed it, and the timing coincided with the GPS reposition so testers attributed it there. The 2 good F3 cases = Q162=Yes path (walks the block naturally). **Also explains the dashboard's dateless F3 cases** (submissions-over-time uses `date_first_visited`). **Fix:** retarget to `skip to SURVEY_TEAM_LEADER_S_NAME` (closing block's first field); Completed default kept. Branch commit `7a8b55f` → merged to main `5104abb` → F3 regenerated → gates (preflight ALL CLEAN · CSEntry strict PASS · Designer Compile Successful) → **v1.1.5 stamped, committed `8727ee8`, pushed, deployed to CSWeb ("deploy succeeded"), itel updated (app list: F3 v1.1.5 2026-07-19)**. #f3-uat patch note posted incl. per-case recovery: the 6 affected cases (…0002/0003/0005/0008 @040340210, …0002/0003 @040341130) are recoverable — update, Modify the case, fill the Closing block, re-sync. Open design item unchanged: postponed/refused/replaced outcomes still `endlevel` before the end GPS forms (no facility GPS on those).

## 2026-07-19 (LATER-2 — tester video triaged: "Field Control section missing" = F3 end-of-case GPS auto-skip on a pre-reposition case, NOT a missing section)

Carl forwarded the tester's screen recording (`Filed Control section missing.mp4`, 32 s, F3 **v1.1.3** on a tablet). Frame-by-frame: the tester completes a full F3 interview — Section L → **"Closing - case end" section present and fully functional** (video itself shows Survey Team Leader's/Enumerator's/Validated/Edited names, both visit dates, Total Visits, Result of First/Final Visit = Completed) → verification photo captured (filename stamped) → **"Accept Case" fires while the tree's last two sections (FC Facility GPS Capture / FC Patient Home GPS) are never entered**. Diagnosis: (1) nothing is structurally missing — F3's survey-team/Field-Control section is *named* "Closing - case end", and the FC GPS sections exist at their NEW end-of-flow position (#157); (2) the tester's case skipped the GPS forms because its GPS was **already captured at case start** — the case was begun on a pre-reposition build (GPS forms were forms 4–5 then), so the capture-once guard + protect() made the engine auto-skip both all-protected forms at the end → Accept appears right after the photo. **The GPS data is in the case, not lost** (server-side confirmation of case `040341130003` optional — one query on the box, or the docs-gate login for the responses CSV). (3) A *fresh* case on v1.1.3 would instead HANG up to 120 s at the end-of-case GPS — the original "so slow" complaint — fixed in v1.1.4 (~1–2 s warm capture, bench-proven). Field guidance: update to v1.1.4/v1.4.4 per the patch notes; "Closing - case end" IS the survey-team section; GPS auto-captures at the very end and its values appear in the case tree. Open items: (a) the already-flagged design gap — postponed/refused/replaced outcomes `endlevel` before the end-GPS forms → no facility GPS on those cases; (b) consider relabeling F3's "Closing - case end" to mention Field Control to end the naming confusion. Frames in `%TEMP%\fc_frames`.

## 2026-07-19 (LATER — Field Control regression sweep after the GPS reposition; tester report triaged)

Testers reported the **survey team section (Field Control) "not functioning properly" after the GPS repositioning**. Full regression pass on the deployed build (main disk state = v1.1.4/v1.4.4 bundles): **no functional defect found in Field Control.** Evidence: (1) **structure** — F1 fmf forms/ordinals/group-bindings fully consistent post-move (FC=11 → Photo=12 → GPS=13 last; F3: Photo → Facility GPS → P_HOME GPS last, matching the warm-radio ReleaseGPS placement), `DataCaptureType` inventory byte-identical to the committed baseline (Date pickers + result pickers intact — the 2026-06-12 capture-type-regression class ruled out); (2) **runtime** — re-ran the previously-passing breakoff desk-tests: F1 BREAKOFF 7 → Result=5 Replaced → clean Accept ✅, F3 BREAKOFF → Result=7 Replaced ✅, F4 BREAKOFF → Result=5 Replaced ✅ + F4 continue-regression stays in questionnaire ✅; (3) **new FC-tail scenario** (BREAKOFF 4 = Incomplete, the no-endlevel path): flows FC → Photo (gated) → **GPS-last form** → "Accept this case?" with no traps/loops ✅; (4) **persistence ("no data leak")** — saved case verified IN the csdb: key=QN correct, `field_control` row (result 4 / breakoff 4 / disposition 2 / language / full geo decomposition) and `rec_facility_capture` values all exact; the row then survived a modify-mode collision + an id-mutation attempt (CSEntry's 92103 id-guard refused it) + a second Add session **unchanged across three reads** — no cross-case bleed. **Most probable cause of the tester report: the v1.1.1–v1.1.3 builds (07-16/17)** — after the reposition the OLD cold `gps(read, 120, 20)` fired at the very END of the flow, right after the survey-team fields, so finishing Field Control looked like a 2-minute freeze (worst indoors). Exactly what v1.1.4 removed → testers must UPDATE per the posted patch notes and re-test; if it persists on v1.1.4 we need the specific symptom + device. **ONE real regression found (design decision, NOT shipped):** since the reposition, Postponed/Refused/Replaced outcomes hit `endlevel` (ENUM_RESULT_FINAL_VISIT not in 1,4) BEFORE the now-last GPS form → **those cases save with NO facility GPS** (pre-#157 every case had it; affects map plotting + replacement tracking). Proposed fix if wanted: take a fresh read on the warm radio in the endlevel branch (~1–2 s) before ending. Desk-only notes (pre-existing, device-N/A): GPS numerics warn 88889 on blank desktop Enter; photo trigger loops on desktop (no camera).

## 2026-07-19 (GPS "so slow" — warm-radio capture pattern in all 4 CAPI apps)

Testers reported GPS capture as very slow. **Root cause** (not a tuning issue): every capture site called `ReadGPSReading(120, 20)` from `shared/Capture-Helpers.apc`, which (1) opened the radio COLD at the capture field, (2) blocked in `gps(read, 120, 20)` while the receiver acquired satellites from scratch — 30–120 s on the Itel tablets, often the full 120 s indoors — and (3) `gps(close)`d immediately, throwing the warm state away so every retry cold-started again. **Fix (warm-radio pattern):** `WarmUpGPS()` — non-blocking `gps(open)` in `PROC QUESTIONNAIRE_NUMBER` **preproc** (F1/F3/F4) so the receiver tracks satellites while the enumerator types the case key + geo IDs; capture reads drop to `ReadGPSReading(15, 20)` (radio warm → fresh fix in ~1–2 s; 15 s only caps the no-signal case); the radio stays open between reads (instant retries; F3's P_HOME block reads warm after the facility block) and `ReleaseGPS()` closes it after each instrument's LAST capture (battery). Every capture is still a **fresh `gps(read)`** — deliberately NO `gps(readlast)` cache (verified against the CSPro 8.0 gps() docs: readlast "might be very old" → a stale fix could silently carry a previous facility's coordinates across cases). Desktop `getos() 10:19` desk-test guard unchanged. SV hub rows: `ReadGPSReading(20, 20)` + release per touchpoint row (sporadic rows, radio may be cold — 20 s cap vs the old 2-min hang). Touched: `shared/Capture-Helpers.apc` (WarmUpGPS/ReleaseGPS + no-close read + `gpsRadioOpen` flag), `F1/procs/control_procs.apc`, `F3/generate_apc.py` (×2 blocks), `F4/generate_apc.py`, `SV/generate_apc.py` + SV tests (3/3 pass), and the three Skip-Logic docs' snippets. Regenerated all four `.ent.apc` (mgf renumbered clean), `preflight_validate.py` ALL CLEAN, **Designer compile-verified 4/4 Successful** (F1 01:34, F3 01:38, F4 01:39, SV 01:39 shots in `automation/shots/`). **Driver trap found on the way** (fixed procedure, not code): `cspro_compile_driver.py`'s `connect(title_re=…)` fast-path attaches to an already-open Designer and its `^l` lands in the logic editor as Scintilla *cut-line* — first pass silently "compiled" F1 three times while eating a header line per run (unsaved buffer only; disk verified intact). Always `taskkill CSPro` between per-instrument runs. **NOT deployed**: this branch's F3/F4 generators are behind main (Section-N/roster hunks — disjoint from the GPS edits, so the merge is clean), so `.apc` built here would regress prod; deploy rides the normal release lane from main after merge. **CSEntry strict gate (the REAL gate per the 2026-06-09 masking trap): F1/F3/F4 all PASS** (`csentry_verify.py`, no `.ent.err`). **ON-DEVICE BENCH PASS (itel P10001L, indoors, 2026-07-19 ~01:52–01:58):** published a fresh F1 `.pen` (Designer File→Publish, driven via keyboard/click automation), sideloaded as a separate `F1-GPS-Bench` pilot dir (prod app dirs untouched), drove a case via adb. Verified end-to-end: (1) **warm-up fires at case start** — Android location service logged `gps provider +registration … WorkSource{gov.census.cspro.csentry}` HIGH_ACCURACY the moment the case opened, status-bar location icon appeared; (2) PSGC gate + barangay cascade work in the pilot (bad prefix hard-stops; NCR/Caloocan QN `138010001001` passes); (3) **GPS capture ≤2 s** — at +2 s after advancing past Interview status the flow was already in Section A with the capture done (old code: up to 120 s blocking); (4) **fix quality: 5 m accuracy indoors** (warm radio had the fix banked; read returned immediately on the ≤20 m target), lat 14.306342 / lon 121.113025 / alt 55.1 m / readtime stamped, all six fields protected; (5) **ReleaseGPS() works** — the status-bar location icon disappeared right after capture. Cleanup: pilot dir + fake bench case deleted from the tablet (the pilot `.pen` carries prod sync config — never leave it on a field device), local bench `.pen` deleted. Full shot series in the session job dir (`gpsbench/b01–b13`).

**MERGED + DEPLOYED (same day, Carl: "merge this to main and deploy to the tablets"):** committed the 19 GPS files on the branch (`c1fb547`), merged into main (`dbfd153`) — main's colliding LOCAL state (live `assignments-source.csv`/`targets.json`, `.gitignore` F2-credential rules, stale `csweb-map-gen.py` working copy, untracked standup) was backed up to the job dir + set aside via tagged stash `gps-merge-set-aside-2026-07-19` (`bcb76de2`, **left in the stash stack** — classifier blocked the drop; safe to `git stash drop` after review), then live assignments/targets + the `.gitignore` rules were restored post-merge. Conflicts: union `.gitignore`; generated F3/F4 artifacts resolved by **regenerating on main from the merged sources** (F4 .apc now carries BOTH main's Section-N rework and the GPS pattern; never trust an auto-merged generated file). Gates on main: SV tests 3/3, preflight ALL CLEAN, CSEntry strict gate F1/F3/F4 PASS. **Versions bumped + stamped v1.1.4/v1.1.4/v1.4.4 (2026-07-19)** (`54484ed`), pushed to origin (main + branch). **Deployed to CSWeb** via `cspro-auto-deploy` lane — drove Designer File→Publish-and-Deploy per instrument (no pre-opened dialogs needed), `auto_deploy.py` checklist verified per dialog (package name, .pff+.ent+8 PSGC files, main dcf only, CSWeb URL), all three "Application Deployed Successfully". **Itel P10001L updated in place** via Add Application→CSWeb→UPDATE ×3 — app list now reads F1 v1.1.4 / F3 v1.1.4 / F4 v1.4.4 (2026-07-19); mid-flow a blind tap opened CSES MODULE C case 4063 in modify mode — backed out with no changes (list still 100 cases). Also confirmed on-screen: main's uncommitted `.fmf` really does have the GPS form LAST (#157), so the warm radio spans the whole interview in prod. NOT deployed: hub/SV apps (own lane; server shows LoginApp update pending from the 2026-07-15 hub v1.1.4 — predates this session). **Remaining field step: other tablets pull the update the same way** (⋮ → Add Application → CSWeb → CONNECT → UPDATE per app) — packages are live server-side. **Announced:** per-instrument patch notes posted to #f1-uat / #f3-uat / #f4-uat (2026-07-19, UPDATE-in-place instructions per the corrected 2026-07-17 propagation finding — NOT the template's remove-first fallback). **Guides updated + redeployed** (per the patch-fix skill's step-7 rule — guides describing old behavior generate phantom bug reports): `docs/enumerator-guide.html` (§7 GPS bullet + troubleshooting row) and `docs/capi-manual.html` §8.1 (4 passages: capture step, blank-GPS warning, caption re-read note, common-problem row) — all now describe search-from-case-start / ~1–2 s capture / 15 s bounded stop / instant retry near a window. Deployed via aspsi-csweb key (prod is LF — scp'd LF-normalized copies; vault copies stay CRLF), box backups at `/root/{enumerator-guide,capi-manual}.html.bak-<ts>`, live-verified by content grep. help.html GPS mentions checked — descriptive only, no staleness.

## 2026-07-17 Sync Dashboard IA redesign — purpose bands + coverage accordion + data-quality panel (DEPLOYED)

**Why.** Carl: "audit this dashboard and arrange them according to its purpose… this long page is bothering me." Audit found the page had accreted 7 feature layers in 10 days with no layout pass: the real F1 plan (1 target per sampled facility) made **Coverage vs. target render 1,521 flat rows ≈ 90% of the scroll** (1,515 reading `1 · 0 · 0% · 1`, rebuilt on every filter change) over just 35 real cases; instrument cards stranded after productivity; quality signals living only in the transient bell; and the coverage banner **mislabelling the real 2026-07-15 pretest plan as "R6 fixture (placeholder)"** (`gen-targets.py --plan-label` default nobody overrode — provenance banner built to prevent mislabels was itself mislabelled).

**Decision trail.** Options diagram (`docs/diagrams/2026-07-17-dashboard-ia-options.png`) → Carl chose **A · purpose bands + rollup**, **region → province two-level** grouping, **detail charts collapsed by default**. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-17-dashboard-ia-design.md`.

**Shipped** (base = the LIVE `/opt/csweb-dashboard-gen.py`, 1,080 lines — never main, per the generator-drift rule):
- **Five purpose bands:** Status now (KPIs + cards moved up + one freshness line) → Progress vs plan (trend + coverage) → Team (productivity) → Data quality (new) → Instrument detail. Team's band label renders inside `renderProductivity` so it vanishes with the panel.
- **Coverage accordion:** region rows → provinces → facility table (column sort kept). Rows with landed>0 auto-open; explicit clicks override and survive re-renders (`covUser` Map outside `render()`). Per-instrument facility search force-opens matches, keeps focus across the innerHTML rebuild. Grouping keys come ONLY from targets' own strings (case rows contribute to landed via code9) — sidesteps the survey-internal region-name mismatch. **Initial page: 3,516px vs ~50,000px before (~93% shorter), F1 = 17 closed region rows.**
- **Data-quality panel:** No-GPS (F2 excluded by design) · Partials >2 days · Completed off-plan (itemised) · Live alerts mirroring the bell's dup-key/off-plan feed via its **own** 20s fetch — the bell IIFE stayed byte-identical (verified: markers present, md5 prod==worktree).
- **Collapsed instrument sections** with `· N charts · M cases in view` headers; charts build lazily on expand (Chart.js can't size a hidden canvas) and are destroyed on collapse; F2's health summary (submissions · last date · f2-api state) lives in the header so an outage can't hide behind a collapsed section.
- **Fixes:** stale plan label → `"Pretest assignment plan 2026-07-15 (provisional)"`; `.enumchip[hidden]{display:none}` (class `display:flex` was beating the `hidden` attribute — the empty pill has been on the page since the chip shipped); footer freshness dedupe; cards click-to-filter.
- **gen-targets.py + assignments-source.csv:** CSV gained `region,province` columns; `build()` falls back to them when the masterlist can't enrich — the F3/F4 pretest EA codes (household EAs / off-sample sites) are structurally absent from `facility_lookup.dat`, so they rendered "(region TBD)". Now `REGION 4-A · Laguna`, matching the masterlist strings exactly. F1 targets byte-identical.

**Verification.** Off-box first: `--sample` fixture from the live payload + local site root + Playwright — accordion, search focus, quality lists, lazy charts, card filter, **zero console errors**. Deploy: prod re-pulled and confirmed unchanged (1,080 lines) → timestamped backups (`/opt/*.bak.20260717-072522`) → scp gen + targets → regenerated **dashboard AND map** on-box (targets.json feeds both; map: 17R/80P, `unmatched=[]`) → live page marker checks ALL PASS → live headless render: 3,516px, zero errors. Worktree copy synced (md5 `3e68d8…` == prod); tracked `targets.json` synced.

**Windows trap (again):** the Edit toolchain flipped the scp'd LF file to full CRLF — caught by byte-count check, converted back before deploy (a CRLF shebang breaks direct execution on the box).

**Deliberately untouched:** bell, map generator, chart set (the Status-doughnut/KPI redundancy is noted in the spec as a future trim, not acted on).

## 2026-07-17 F1/F3/F4 paradata was never collected — `.pff` `Paradata=` line added + SHIPPED (#840)

**Finding.** #840 stated *"paradata collection is enabled … the durations are on the enumerators' devices."* **False.** `automation/cspro_compile_driver.py:125` writes `"paradata": {"collection": "all"}` into each `.ent` — but CSPro only writes the log if the **`.pff` `[Files]`** section also carries a **`Paradata=`** line saying where. None of our three `.pff` had one, so the setting was **INERT: zero paradata has ever been collected for F1/F3/F4, on any device.** Option B in the issue ("use the paradata already collected") was a non-option. **The 15–17 Jul pretest interview durations were never recorded and are unrecoverable.**

**Evidence (itel P10001L).** Natural experiment, one device, same CSEntry, one variable: the 5 CSES 2024 tutorial apps each carry `Paradata=` in their `.pff` and each has a `.cslog`; F1/F3/F4 had neither (5/5 vs 0/3). Verified causally — a `.pff` with the line → `FacilityHeadSurvey.cslog` (282 KB) appeared within seconds of opening F1, full paradata schema (`case_event`, `field_entry_event`, `event`, `session_event`, `gps_event`).

**Shipped (Option C).** `automation/add_paradata_pff.py` — idempotent, preserves the `.pff` UTF-8 BOM + CRLF, inserts after `InputData=`. Bump-safe: `stamp_version.py stamp_pff()` only rewrites `Description=` in `[Run Information]` (confirmed — the line survived two bumps). **`.pff`-only change: no `.dcf`/`.fmf`/`.apc`, so NOT covered by the pretest dictionary freeze.** Deployed **F1 v1.1.3 · F3 v1.1.3 · F4 v1.4.3**; preflight ALL CLEAN (no Designer recompile — nothing compilable changed, and CSDeploy recompiles `.ent`→`.pen` at the Deploy click anyway).

**Verified end-to-end, not inferred.** Updated all three on the itel **from CSWeb** → deployed `.pff` carries both `Description=… v1.1.3` (proving it came from the deploy, not a push) and `Paradata=`. Opened F3 → `PatientSurvey.cslog` created. **The `.cslog` survives the update flow** (F1's log came through v1.1.2→v1.1.3 intact). Patch notes posted to #capi-scrum + #f1/#f3/#f4-uat, flagging the version-to-check change (v1.1.1/v1.4.1 → v1.1.3/v1.4.3). Paradata now collects → **18–20 Jul durations will be captured.**

**⚠ TRAP — never `adb push` into a CSEntry app dir.** Mid-run every update began failing with *"Error extracting application. The application package may be invalid or their may be insufficent disk space"* (disk was 8% used / 101 GB free). **Cause: my own `adb push`** — it writes a **`shell`-owned** file into `/sdcard/Android/data/gov.census.cspro.csentry/files/csentry/<App>/`, and CSEntry (running as **`u0_a223`**) cannot overwrite it, so extraction fails on write. It presents *exactly* like a corrupt package. I misattributed it to the `Paradata=` change and reverted + redeployed all three — **the revert did not fix it, which is what exposed the real cause**; deleting the pushed `.pff` + `.bak` fixed it instantly. **The package and CSWeb were fine throughout and the field was never at risk** — the fault existed only on the test device and was created by the debugging step. Lesson: a self-inflicted device artifact can perfectly mimic a broken deploy; trusting the first diagnosis would have discarded a working change. (Also: `adb push` to `/sdcard/...` from git-bash mangles the remote path → prefix `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1`.)

**Pattern worth a sweep — second instance today.** Same shape as the F1/F3/F4 validation docs asserting Philippine bounding-box HARD checks that don't exist in code: **a setting declared in one artifact, never wired in the other, and nobody checked the artifact it should produce.** Worth auditing what else is "enabled" but inert.

**Still open on #840** (kept OPEN): (1) **retrieval** — the `.cslog` stays on-device, it does NOT ride the `direction: put` case sync; getting durations off the tablets is a separate step (adb pull / CSPro paradata sync), nothing collects them automatically yet. (2) **Option A** — the off-form `INTERVIEW_TIMING` block (`START_TIME`/`END_TIME`/`DURATION_MIN`) whose duration rides the case sync into CSWeb remains the durable reporting answer; post-pretest (dict change). Diagram: `docs/diagrams/2026-07-17-interview-duration-840.png`.

## 2026-07-16 F1/F3/F4 GPS moved after the verification photo — live-pretest unblock (#157, DEPLOYED)

**What / why.** Pretest field teams (Los Baños & Bay, `from-pretest-2026-07`) reported they "cannot proceed" with Facility Head / Patient interviews — no GPS signal inside hospitals. They asked for GPS at the end of the survey. Carl's call: put GPS **after the verification photo** in all three instruments. Since the photo was *already* the last form everywhere (F1 `FORM012` / F3 `FORM038` / F4 `FORM054`), "after the photo" = **GPS becomes dead last** — exactly what the field asked for.

**Root cause (narrower than "blocked").** `shared/Capture-Helpers.apc:42` → `ReadGPSReading(120, 20)` calls `gps(read, maxTimeSec=120, ...)`, which is **synchronous**. Indoors it stalls the enumerator for a full **120s**, then `errmsg`s and leaves the fields blank. Nothing hard-blocks (no retry loop, no required-field trap, and the Philippine bounding-box HARD checks the validation docs claim **do not exist in code**) — but the tablet looks frozen. It fired `onfocus` from an early, **pre-consent** form: F1 `FORM002` (3rd form), F3 `FORM003`+`FORM004` (before consent — **2×120s per patient**), F4 `FORM002` (fused with Geo ID).

**Scope: Option A, move-only. No `.dcf` touched.** Form order is already decoupled from dict order here (F4's `FIELD_CONTROL` items 1–9 render ~51 forms *after* items 18–20; F1's `REC_FACILITY_CAPTURE` already split GPS/photo across two far-apart forms). So: no item reorder, no start-position shift, **no migration — synced + in-progress cases unaffected** (case data binds to dict items, not form positions; live path is `.csdb`/SQLite anyway). Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-16-gps-placement-design.md`. Diagram: `docs/diagrams/2026-07-16-gps-placement.excalidraw`.

**Changes.**
- **F3** `generate_fmf.py` — moved both GPS tuples (`FC Facility GPS Capture`, `FC Patient Home GPS`) after `Case Verification Photo` in `FORM_PLAN`.
- **F4** `generate_fmf.py` — new `HH_GPS_ITEMS` constant (single source of truth); `HOUSEHOLD_GEO_ID` on the geo form now excludes items 7–12, and a new **`FC HH GPS Capture`** form after the photo carries only those. Geo form keeps CLASSIFICATION / BARANGAY / HH_ADDRESS. (`_filter_items` takes `names` OR `exclude`, not both — hence the combined exclude list.)
- **F1** — no `generate_fmf.py`; added **`F1/inject_gps_end.py`** (post-processor modeled on `inject_field_control_end.py`): moves the `Facility GPS` `[Form]`+`[Group]` after the photo, re-derives every form ordinal from position. Pipeline is now `fmf_checkbox_convert → inject_blocks → inject_case_key → inject_field_control_end → inject_gps_end`. Verified idempotent.
- **`F1/inject_field_control_end.py` — INVARIANT RELAXED (important).** It hard-asserted *"Verification Photo is no longer the last form — aborting"*, which would have broken the pipeline on **every future re-run** once GPS sat after the photo. Its guard is now position-independent and enforces the invariant that actually matters: **Field Control immediately BEFORE the photo** (the photo's `preproc` gates on `ENUM_RESULT_FINAL_VISIT`, which FC collects). Do not re-add a "photo must be last" assertion.

**Resolved risk:** F1's GPS PROC still precedes the photo PROC in the `.apc` (`generate_apc.py` derives PROC order from the dictionary, not the form plan) while GPS is now the last form — **CSPro does not require PROC order to match form order**; F1 compiled clean. No generator reordering needed. The F1 GPS group carries no `[Block]` entries, so block Position math was untouched.

**Gates.** `preflight_validate` ALL CLEAN · `verify_questions` F1 316/316 · F3 370/370 · F4 299/299 reachable, 0 dead-conditions, 0 bad-skips, **PASS** ×3 · `fmf_block_check` **[ OK ]** ×3. Fresh-Designer compile (`cspro_compile_driver --build`): **Compile Successful** ×3 (F1 21:36:37, F3 21:37:47, F4 21:39:03), form trees confirm GPS last. 3-step FMF promotion run for F3/F4 (capture types re-optimised — DropDowns + date pickers intact).

**Deploy.** `stamp_version.py bump` (PATCH) → **F1 v1.1.1 · F3 v1.1.1 · F4 v1.4.1** (2026-07-16), no drift. Deployed all three via the `.csds` spec route (`open_csdeploy_spec` → `auto_deploy --deploy-only`); "Application Deployed Successfully" confirmed to `https://csweb.asiansocial.org/csweb/api`; shots in `automation/shots/deploy/`. Carl gave standing autodeploy authorisation for this pass.

**Announced.** Short patch notes posted to #f1-uat / #f3-uat / #f4-uat with the new build version + **remove-and-re-add** instructions (⋮ → Update Installed Applications is unreliable — the real rollout hazard here is propagation, not the code).

**Honest limits.** The 120s stall is **RELOCATED, not removed** — if the enumerator stays indoors at case-close it still stalls, but the respondent is already done. This deliberately reverses the 2026-06-12 rule *"GPS stays early so it auto-locks while the form is worked"* (comment updated in both generators); indoors that assumption never held.

**Follow-ups (not in this change).** (1) `GPS_UNAVAILABLE_REASON` reason code (Option B) — needs a `.dcf` change + re-register + breakout rebuild; land after the pretest window closes. Today a failed fix leaves silent blanks and the Map Report counts them as `nofix` with no reason. (2) **Stale validation docs** — `F1/F1-Skip-Logic-and-Validations.md:171-179` (+ F3/F4 equivalents) document a `FACILITY_CAPTURE_GPS` trigger removed 2026-06-12 and bounding-box HARD checks that don't exist. (3) **Next round's tester guide** must not copy R5 step `5C.3` *"the verification photo is the very last step"* — GPS is now after it (the June R4/R5 guides are left as historical round artifacts). (4) F3 `P_HOME_GPS` — if captured while standing in the facility it records the facility's coordinates, making the map-gen `displacement` flag meaningless. (5) `facility_lookup.dcf` carries **no coordinates**, so there's no reference to validate a fix against and no fallback pre-fill.

## 2026-07-16 F2 Model C — Numbered self-register links (design + full-stack build, deploy-pending)

**Deliverable (F2 Model C numbered links).** Re-engineered F2 self-service from the single facility-QR self-register into **per-HCW numbered short-links**: `uhc-hcw.asiansocial.org/e/<CODE>-HCW-NN?k=<secret>` (readable slug + unguessable 6-char secret). Opening the link verifies slug+secret server-side, mints a QN-bound device token, and drops the HCW straight into consent→survey — no token paste, no HCW-ID picker. Replaces the facility-QR handout as the PRIMARY distribution (that path is kept for shared-device/walk-ins). Personal-device assumption (one device → one QN).

**Built end-to-end + tested, then DEPLOYED to prod 2026-07-16** for the pretest (Carl authorised the full deploy despite the earlier "held frontend" posture). Verified: **80/80 server tests, 528/528 app tests, clean `tsc -b` + eslint**, prod build clean, smoke green (`/claim` + `/admin/api/facility-links` → 401 live-and-gated; `enroll_slug`/`enroll_secret_hmac`/`claimed_at` confirmed on `f2_hcws`). Shipped the whole app working tree (Model C + the entangled QN plumbing it needs + already-staged tested UI changes) via `pretest-2026-07-16/deploy_model_c_full.sh` (backend → idempotent DDL → frontend to `/opt/app/f2-www`, live off the ro-mount).
- **Backend** (`deliverables/F2/PWA/server/`): DDL adds `enroll_slug` / `enroll_secret_hmac` (keyed HMAC, never plaintext) / `claimed_at` to `f2_hcws`; `POST /claim {slug,k}`; `POST /admin/api/facility-links` (dash_users) stamps `HCW-01..NN` + fresh secrets shown once; helpers in `src/enroll-links.ts`; tests `test/claim.test.ts` + `test/admin.test.ts`.
- **Frontend** (`app/`): `components/enrollment/ClaimScreen.tsx` + `lib/claim-client.ts` (`App.tsx` routes `/e/<slug>` when unenrolled); admin `data/FacilityLinksModal.tsx` behind a "Facility links" button on the HCWs tab; `claim` i18n block across all 8 locales (en real, 7 English-fallback).
- **Design doc:** `deliverables/F2/F2-Model-C-Numbered-Links-Design-2026-07-16.md` (carries the as-built file list); supersedes-as-primary `F2-Model-C-Self-Register-Design-2026-07-16.md`. Project memory `project_aspsi_f2_model_c_numbered_links` added.

## 2026-07-13 LATER² (FIRST LIVE ETL RUN — pipeline proven; the DATA is the problem)

Ran the new ETL against the box (read-only). **The pipeline works end-to-end. What it found
is a data-state report, not a code report.**

**Pipeline proven:**
- **F3: 9 cases × 343 columns + 9 pay-rosters AUTO-DISCOVERED** (`q92/q94_lab/q971/q972/q98/
  q107/q109/q112/q113`). The generic `occ` detection picked up the 2026-06-20 roster fan-out
  with zero code changes — exactly what it was built for. (Column count fell 809 → 343 because
  the cost matrices MOVED OUT of the section tables into roster children. Correct.)
- **F2: 41 submissions × 173 columns (151 answers) — in the master dataset for the first time.**
  Richest submission carries 130 real answers (`Q3=Female`, `Q5=Nurse`, `Q4=35`). 2 refusals
  (#825) carried as data.

**What the data says (all verified against CSWeb's own store, `csweb_uhc_y2`):**
| instrument | cases on server | live |
|---|---|---|
| F1 | 2 | **0 — both deleted** |
| F3 | 12 | 9 |
| F4 | **0 — none exist at all** | — |
- **10 hard-gate failures, all TRUE:** F4 absent entirely (1) + 9 F3 cases whose facility block
  has no live F1 case (because F1's two cases were deleted). The gates are doing their job.
- **ALL 41 F2 submissions have NO questionnaire number.** Every one carries a legacy SLUG
  facility (`DEMO-FAC-RHU-QC-1`, `FAC-002`) — and auto-QN only fires on a 9-digit PSGC code.
  So none of them can join to F1/F3/F4 on `case_key`. **The QN assignment path has never run
  against a real enrollment** — which is precisely what P5's QN leg (`p5-qn-check` @ 040340002
  → expect `040340002001`) exists to prove. 36 of the 41 are also on the pre-R6 spec
  (2026-04-17-m1) — April UAT data.

**Two bugs of mine, caught by live data and fixed + regression-tested:**
1. **F2 `sex` never reached the join layer** — F2's key is `Q3` with a *label* value (`Female`),
   not the CAPI-style `q*_sex` code. Now recoded to the canonical `1/2` (codebook §3), so F2
   and F3 finally speak one language in `shared_dimensions.csv` (39 F2 sex rows).
2. **`region_code` was garbage for F2** — I derived it by slicing `facility_id`, but a slug
   sliced to 2 chars yields region **`DE`**, silently poisoning every cross-instrument
   geography join. Now: region is derived ONLY from a numeric 9-digit code. Verified: region
   values across the whole dataset are `{04, 19}`, no junk.

**One assumption I got wrong and corrected before shipping:** I'd written the extract to treat a
0-byte dump as a failed query ("an empty table would still print a header"). **The box says
otherwise** — `mysql --batch` prints *nothing at all* for an empty result set. Had that shipped,
every legitimately-empty table (`notes`, an unused roster, F4 today) would have hard-crashed the
extract. Reverted; the real protection is the QA gate (instrument absent → hard stop), not an
extract error. **22 tests green.**

## 2026-07-13 LATER (ETL: 13 columns → the whole survey; F2 WIRED IN)

**The master-dataset gap is closed in code.** `transform.py` rewritten from a 13-column
shared-dimension subset to full-variable extraction across all four instruments, and F2 —
which had **no path into the ETL at all** — is now wired in.

- **CAPI wide-join:** every section table (`a_*`, `b_*`, …) merges onto one case row. Safe
  because CSPro guarantees item names unique *within* a dictionary — verified 682/821/645
  unique columns across F1/F3/F4, **zero collisions**. Output went **13 → 676 / 809 / 606
  columns**. Breakout plumbing (`*-id`, `occ`, `level-1-id`) stripped from the payload.
- **Rosters discovered by the `occ` column**, not hard-coded — so the F3 `*_PAY_ROSTER`
  tables from the 2026-06-20 fan-out are picked up with no code change (the Jun-12 raw
  predates them). Emitted as `<inst>__<roster>.csv` keyed by case_key + occ.
- **F2 (`transform_f2`)**: reads `csweb_f2.f2_responses` (the live store since P4), explodes
  `values_json` into columns union-ed across submissions (spec changes add columns instead of
  breaking the ETL). `case_key` = the 12-digit `qn`; pre-QN rows keep facility_id, get a blank
  key + QA flag (they cannot join to F1/F3/F4). Refusals (#825) flow through as data.
- **Two real bugs caught by writing it:** (a) `load_table` never un-escaped `mysql --batch`
  output — tab/newline/backslash inside `values_json` would have made every F2 blob
  unparseable and silently lost **every F2 answer**; (b) `write_csv` used `rows[0]`'s keys, so
  ragged rows would drop columns. Both fixed, both regression-tested.
- **Security:** `csweb_f2` also holds `f2_users` (PBKDF2 hashes) + `auth_*` (device/session
  tokens). The extract **whitelists** survey tables (`f2_responses`/`f2_hcws`/
  `f2_facility_master`/`f2_dlq`), and re-checks after unpacking — a denied table appearing in
  `raw/` is deleted and the run fails loudly.
- **`etl/test_transform.py` — NEW, 20 tests, stdlib, all green** (escaping round-trip,
  refusal-as-data, pre-QN blank key, paper-encoded provenance, wide join, roster discovery,
  rectangular CSV). The ETL had zero tests before.
- **Verified end-to-end** against the Jun-12 raw: 0 hard-gate failures, real answers present
  (`q1_is_patient`, `q2_relationship`, `q4_name`…), roster child file emitted. NOTE the Jun-12
  snapshot is thin/stale (4 desk-test cases, mostly empty shells, pre-roster-fan-out) — it
  proves the pipeline, it is not a data sample. **First meaningful run = the pretest.**
- **etl-spec §2.2 REPOINTED** (this was the P6 prerequisite): the old "R2 CSV break-out
  (preferred)" is now **dangerous** — the Worker's */5 cron kept minting fresh-dated CSVs from
  the FROZEN Sheet after P4, so anything reading them ingests stale data. Direct `csweb_f2`
  SQL replaces it; the bucket dies at P6.
- Codebook v0.6. **Deliberately NOT built:** the `.dta` writer — blocked on ASPSI answering
  the handoff-format question (and 18 F1 + 9 F4 column names exceed Stata's 32-char limit;
  reported in `qa_report.md`, never silently truncated). `manifest.json` records
  `"weights_applied": false` so nobody downstream assumes otherwise.

The review earned its keep: the 3 parallel build agents produced 4 Postgres artifacts that **drifted badly** (roles the poller/dashboard log in as never created by the schema; service-name mismatch → F2 renders empty; two contradictory geo views; two poller scripts; `gps` computed 3 ways = 100% false "No GPS fix"; and `POSTGRES_USER=f2_app` = the internet-adjacent writer becomes a **superuser/RCE** — critical). Rather than patch 31 Postgres findings, diagrammed the real fork (`f2-store-scope-decision.png` then `-mysql.png`, per diagram-before-deciding) and Carl chose **MySQL read-mirror**, which **dissolves most of the findings** (no new container/roles/cluster ⇒ no superuser landmine, no host↔container connectivity gap; no write path ⇒ no f2_config/f2_hcws/dead-letter/batch gaps). **Built (single-author, inline — the drift lesson):**
- **`csweb_f2_schema.sql`** (MySQL 8) — new `csweb_f2` DB in the SAME `lamp-mysql8` container: `f2_responses` (18 sheet cols + `synced_at` freshness, JSON `values_json`, UNIQUE `client_submission_id`) + `f2_facility_master` (facility→area NAMES for the by-NAME rollup; empty until seeded PSGC-identically = deferred geo). Lenient columns (no CHECK on client-influenced fields) so a mirror never silently drops a Sheet-accepted row.
- **`sync_f2_to_mysql.py`** — the poller (supersedes the Postgres draft): Apps Script `admin_read_responses` RPC (HMAC envelope preserved verbatim from the workflow's reverse-engineering) → `INSERT … ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE` into `csweb_f2`. Reuses the dashboard's **`docker compose exec database mysql`** primitive (zero new host deps beyond `requests`, sidesteps the connectivity finding); untrusted values passed as **MySQL hex literals** (`_utf8mb4 0x…`) = injection-proof + byte-exact multibyte; per-row isolation on batch error, non-zero exit for cron alerting; `--backfill`/`--since auto` watermark. `py_compile` + stubbed smoke test PASS (hex-encode, tz-convert, keyless-drop, 18-col row all correct).
- **`csweb-dashboard-gen.py`** patched **inline** — F2 is a 4th section/instrument reading `csweb_f2` via the existing `q()` path. New `F2_SQL` (status='Completed'/gps='1' FIXED by design — F2 has no partial-save + no GPS, so it never inflates Partial/No-GPS; `submitted_at_server` UTC→Manila for trend/today); `SECTIONS`+`QUERIES` gain `f2` (charts: Disposition Submitted/Refusal · Region · Capture Mode); client JS generalized `['f1','f3','f4']`→`INSTS=P.spec.map(prefix)` (dropdown/cards/KPIs/coverage all in sync); 4-col card grid; **F2 freshness note** (surfaces mirror state — "N mirrored · last submission …" or "No F2 submissions mirrored yet" = no silent flatline).
- **`F2-MySQL-Readmirror-Runbook.md`** — Phase A (stand up DB → backfill poller → 2-min cron → ship generator; each `[Carl-gated]`/`[automatable]` + GATE + rollback); §4 what it does NOT change (live write path, geo deferred, gps by design); **§7 Phase B = full primary cutover DEFERRED + Carl-gated** (dual-write→backfill config/enrolment→primary flip→provenance-preserving rollback→resolve box-vs-Elestio end-state). Secrets named only (`APPS_SCRIPT_URL`/`APPS_SCRIPT_HMAC`/`MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD` in a **disjoint** `/opt/f2sync/.env`) with the HMAC-master-scope security note.

Real-Chart.js pixel preview rendered + sent to Carl (F2 doughnuts + region bar + freshness note; shipped HTML still vendored, no CDN). **Graceful-degrade** verified: F2-absent fixture → F2 card 0 + "No F2 submissions mirrored yet" note, F1/F3/F4 KPI total=13 unaffected, 0 errors. Removed the 4 superseded Postgres artifacts (schema/compose/poller/runbook) to prevent an operator running the wrong engine. **Then ran a SECOND ultracode Workflow (20 agents) to adversarially review the FRESH MySQL artifacts** (they'd only been headless-tested; the 31 findings were vs the Postgres design) — verify-contract (3, vs the real F2 backend source) → 5 review lenses → per-finding adversarial confirm. **Core clean:** the data-model + F1/F3/F4-regression contract agents returned ZERO findings (the `INSTS` refactor + F2 integration are sound). **9 CONFIRMED (all operational-edge, none in F2 correctness) + fixed inline + re-verified:** (poller) `_lit_num` non-finite floats (`Infinity`/`NaN`/`1e400`→bare `inf`/`nan` = invalid SQL) now `math.isfinite`→NULL; MySQL root pw moved OFF argv (`ps`-readable) → `-e MYSQL_PWD` env; `subprocess.run` given `timeout=120`+`TimeoutExpired`→RuntimeError (a wedged mysql was holding the flock forever); `synced_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP` appended to the ODKU so a no-op re-see still bumps it (freshness heartbeat was frozen); SCAN_CAP warning corrected (AS reads OLDEST 50k then filters → newest unreachable past 50k; date-window paging can't recover them — latent, sheet ~30k, backend tail-read fix flagged). ## 2026-07-02 (BUILD VERSIONING SHIPPED — v1.0.0 on all three instruments, tester-visible, deployed)

Testers asked for a visible version number in the CSEntry apps (pre-test readiness + build tracking across UAT rounds) and a way to know whether an installed build is current. CSEntry has NO native app-version concept (the `.ent` version fields are file-format versions — the root of the ⋮-Update unreliability), so built our own, generator-first: **`deliverables/CSPro/versions.json` = single source of truth** (per-instrument `{app, version, date}`; **Semantic Versioning MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH per Carl — PATCH per bug-fix deploy, MINOR for new/changed functionality, MAJOR at UAT round close or a breaking change**; first cut was two-part "v1.0", corrected to semver + redeployed ×3 the same hour), stamped onto two surfaces that ride every CSWeb deploy: (1) **`.pff [Run Information] Description`** → CSEntry's app-list entry + case-listing title (key verified in CSPro source: `PFF.cpp APPDESCRIPTION = "Description"`, displayed verbatim by CSEntryDroid) — written by new **`automation/stamp_version.py`** (show = drift check pff+qsf vs json · stamp · bump/set = update json + restamp pff + regen qsf atomically; BOM-aware, pffs are UTF-8-BOM); (2) **build footer on the first cover question** (SURVEY_TEAM_LEADER_S_NAME — the case-key field has no qsf entry) emitted by each `generate_qsf.py` reading versions.json — `Build: F<n> v1.0.0 (2026-07-02)`, instruction style, every language, so **every bug screenshot identifies its build**. Regens surfaced two stale-QSF catch-ups (same class as yesterday's F3 finds): **F1 BREAKOFF + CASE_DISPOSITION question text** (fields shipped #744 with blank prompts) and **F4 Q84_WHERE_ASSIST_OTHER_TXT**. Gates: preflight ALL CLEAN · verify_questions F1 316/316 · F3 370/370 · F4 381/381 PASS · fmf_block_check F1/F3 OK — **F4 FAILS with 26 Section-N block-position problems, but identically on committed HEAD: PRE-EXISTING, not from this change**; that exact fmf has passed Designer publish + CSDeploy pen-compile repeatedly since Jun-20 (Designer would renumber on save if it disagreed) → checker rule gap on the Section N expenditures grid (expected-position drift +1→+3, likely an uncounted field class), follow-up = reconcile `fmf_block_check` — flagged, not papered over. **Deployed all three via the .csds spec route**: authored `F1/FacilityHeadSurvey.csds` + `F4/HouseholdSurvey.csds` (F3's from yesterday); new **`automation/open_csdeploy_spec.py`** launches CSDeploy.exe fresh (no CLI args exist — Program.cs) + Ctrl+O-loads a spec + verifies the retitle; then `auto_deploy.py F<n> --deploy-only` → **"Application Deployed Successfully" ×3 (F3, F1, F4), 2026-07-02** (+ ×3 again for the semver restamp — six clean deploys through the spec route today). Also verified in CSDeploy source (`PackageBuilder.cs`): the deploy click compiles the `.ent → .pen` fresh from disk (`/pen /binaryName`) — so qsf/pff-only patches deploy with NO Designer step, and a compile failure would hard-stop the deploy (the real gate ran ×3). Side benefit: all three deploy dialogs are now parked minimized WITH loaded specs (Carl's 3-dialog setup, deterministic). **Patch notes posted to all three channels on Carl's go** (v1.0.0; F3's notes it bundles the morning's Section J fix — one remove+re-add gets both). **Versioning documented on Carl's ask: `deliverables/CSPro/VERSIONING.md`** — canonical doc (why no native version exists, the SemVer table, both display surfaces, stamp_version.py commands, release workflow, version-history table to keep current per deploy day). Tester up-to-dateness mechanism: app-list version vs the latest patch note's stated build (every future note states its version — added to the patch-fix skill). Supervisor hub versioning = follow-up (rides its next deploy). On-device retest pending: remove + re-add → app list shows "… - v1.0.0 (2026-07-02)", first cover screen shows the build line. No git (Carl handles).

**Same day — DEVICE-VERIFIED + v1.0.1 placement fix (Carl: "give me a sample image").** Ran the real tester flow on the itel P10001L: ⋮ → Update Installed Applications **detected all of today's redeploys** — after dismissing a crash caused by an UNRELATED app (*the CSES 2024 tutorial apps carry a stale DROPBOX token; the update-all check hits it first — "Unexpected char 0x0a in Bearer {access_token: sl.…}" — tap OK and the update list still appears*). Per-app UPDATE installed cleanly (case data preserved). **App list confirmed: "Patient Survey (F3) - v1.0.0 (2026-07-02)" etc., and the pff Description also renders as the in-case TITLE BAR on every screen** (a third surface — every bug screenshot carries the build regardless of where it's taken). Walking a demo case (QN 040340002901, discarded after) exposed a **placement flaw: the qsf footer rode the DICT-first cover question (SURVEY_TEAM_LEADER_S_NAME), but F3's form puts that cover/FC block at case-END** — testers would never see it up front, and the patch note's "first cover screen" claim was wrong on-device. **Fixed at generator level (v1.0.1):** each generate_qsf.py now emits the footer on the `QUESTIONNAIRE_NUMBER` **id item** (ids never ride the records loop — emitted explicitly), i.e. the guaranteed FIRST screen of every case in all three instruments. ## 2026-06-30 (Supervisor hub — C4b: "View my report" → HTML table via htmldialog())

Carl asked to turn the enumerator/supervisor "View my report" from one run-on `errmsg` paragraph into a real list. Diagrammed the fork (`supervisor-hub/report-format-options.excalidraw/.png`, via diagram-before-deciding): A = structured one line (compile-safe, not separate lines), B = newline in errmsg (chr(10) already failed; render unverified), C = HTMLDialogs table. **Carl chose C.** Grounded the CSPro-8 API at csprousers.org (`htmldialog_function.html`): `htmldialog(file, inputData:=, displayOptions:=)` returns the string the HTML passes to `CS.UI.close()`; HTML reads input via `CS.UI.getInputData()`. Repo-wide search found NO existing htmldialog usage (the Khurshid 109 reference has the HTML but not the .apc), so this is the unbuilt C8 spike. **De-risked the biggest unknown (deps) by writing a SELF-CONTAINED `supervisor-hub/report.html`** — inline CSS + vanilla JS, 0 external src/href (no jQuery/Bootstrap/Mustache to bundle, renders offline), with a CS.UI→deprecated-CSPro fallback shim; reads `{scope,f1,f3,f4,instr,target,ea}` JSON, builds a per-instrument Done/Target table + assignment line. **Generator-first:** rewrote `build_hub_apps.py` `show_coverage_report()` to count F1/F3/F4 via forcase, read MyAssignment.dat, assemble the JSON with `maketext('{...}', …)` (single-quoted format so inner double-quotes are literal — the doc's idiom), call `htmldialog("report.html", …)`, and **fall back to the proven one-line errmsg if it returns != "ok"** (so an unsupported/older CSEntry degrades gracefully, never breaks). Added `report.html` to the deploy bundle. Regenerated; static-verified: htmldialog call at MenuApp.ent.apc:214 wired into both report_sup + report_enum, report.html self-contained. **COMPILE PASSED 2026-07-01** — fresh-Designer MenuApp compile (`cspro_compile_driver.py MENU --save`) = "Compile Successful at 11:08:08" (`automation/shots/MENU_compile.png`), so `htmldialog(file, inputData:=, displayOptions:=)` + the single-quoted `maketext` JSON + the `res <> "ok"` fallback are all valid CSPro 8. **STRICT PUBLISH + DEPLOY PASSED → LIVE ON PROD 2026-07-01** (Carl "deploy to prod"): drove `csweb_deploy_designer.py` (DEPLOY_KEY=LOGIN) open→filemenu→click 115 338 Publish-and-Deploy — the **strict packager accepted htmldialog** (deploy dialog opened, Package=LoginApp, target `csweb.asiansocial.org/csweb/api`, NO "Compile Failed") → `deploy_hub_bundle.py --deploy` staged all 22 files (incl. `report.html`) → **"Application Deployed Successfully"** (`shots/deploy/hub_deploy_now.png`; the ~32MB upload outran the driver's watch window, confirmed on re-check + dismissed the popup + parked the dialog). The htmldialog report is LIVE. **→ DEVICE-VERIFIED WORKING END-TO-END 2026-07-01 (itel_P10001L, C8 spike CONFIRMED):** `htmldialog` renders the HTML **table** on CSEntry with the REAL `forcase` counts (title "MY INTERVIEW COVERAGE", F3 Patient: 2) + OK returns to the menu with **no double-dialog**. Two bugs found + fixed on-device via fast adb-push iteration (report.html is a runtime asset; CSEntry also runs MenuApp from source so pushed .apc recompiles on load): (1) **this CSEntry uses the DEPRECATED `CSPro.*` Action-Invoker interface, NOT modern `CS.UI`** (`CS`/`CS.UI` are undefined) — input = `CSPro.getInputData()` (returns an OBJECT, not a string → handle both), and `CSPro` is a **bare global, not `window.CSPro`** (guard with `typeof`); report.html made robust (tries all APIs, string-or-object). (2) htmldialog's return value on this CSEntry is not a stable "ok" (deprecated `CSPro.returnData` path), so the `if res <> "ok"` **errmsg fallback fired a SECOND dialog every time** → removed the fallback in the generator (render is reliable here). Diagnosed via an on-device probe that dumped `CSPro.fns` (do, getAsyncResult, getInputData, invoke, returnData, runLogic, setDisplayOptions, …). **⚠ CSWeb package caveat:** the *re-deploy* of the fix produced a package the itel rejects on extract ("application package may be invalid" — NOT disk space, 101 GB free); the FIRST deploy was fine, so the redeploy glitched. Device-verified via adb-push of the corrected files. **→ RESOLVED + FULLY SHIPPED 2026-07-01 (Carl "try remove and re-add"):** the repeated "invalid package" on UPDATE was a **poisoned device update-state** (a failed first extraction corrupted the update path), NOT a bad CSWeb package. Removed LoginApp (deleted the on-device app folder) → **Add Application → CSWeb → INSTALL (fresh) EXTRACTED CLEANLY** — so the CSWeb package was valid all along. Re-tested from the REAL CSWeb install (not adb-push): login se-001 → View my report → **HTML table renders with live counts (F3 Patient: 2)** → OK → **clean menu loop, no double-dialog**. C4b is DONE end-to-end from the deployed package. **Field note:** if a tester hits "invalid package" on Update, the fix is **remove + re-add** (already the project's standing update guidance vs the unreliable ⋮ Update — [[reference_csentry_update_propagation]]). Device left logged out, auto-rotate restored. No git (Carl handles).

## 2026-06-29 (Patch — Supervisor hub: Bluetooth-off warning + Receive-label fix; deployed + device-verified)

Two findings from the 2-tablet Bluetooth smoke test (see `deliverables/CSPro/supervisor-hub/bt-smoke-2026-06-29/`) fixed generator-first in `build_hub_apps.py` and shipped to the live hub.

- **Fix #2 — Bluetooth-off gives no clear warning.** During the smoke test, every enumerator scan came back empty because the A23's Bluetooth was simply OFF; the hub only showed an empty "Searching for nearby devices" then a generic "no supervisor host found" — never naming the actual cause. **Root cause:** CSPro logic can't read the BT-adapter state, so detection isn't possible; the fix is message guidance. Broadened the two CLIENT-side `syncconnect` failure messages (`receive_assignment`, `send_to_supervisor`) to **"Couldn't connect over Bluetooth. Check: (1) Bluetooth is ON on BOTH tablets, and (2) the supervisor has started '<Assign EA / Collect>' - then retry."** and prepended a "Make sure Bluetooth is ON on both tablets" reminder to the two HOST-side start messages (`assign_ea`, `collect_interviews`).
- **Fix #3 — hardcoded "(Patient)" menu label.** The enumerator menu item read "Receive Assigned Data (Patient)" even for an F1-assigned enumerator (the label is a static accept()-option string, not derived from the runtime assignment). Changed to the instrument-agnostic **"Receive Assigned Data"** (matches the supervisor's "Assign Enumeration Area", which carries no instrument suffix).

**Gates:** regen (`py build_hub_apps.py`) → confirmed in generated `MenuApp.ent.apc` (4 BT-on hits + label) → **fresh-Designer compile MenuApp = "Compile Successful at 21:18:54"** → **LoginApp strict-publish PASS** (deploy dialog opened, Package=LoginApp, target `csweb.asiansocial.org/csweb/api`). **Deploy:** closed leftover deploy dialogs (none) → `csweb_deploy_designer.py`(DEPLOY_KEY=LOGIN) open→filemenu→click 115 338 → `deploy_hub_bundle.py --deploy` staged all 21 files → **"Application Deployed Successfully"** (the ~32MB upload outran the 24s watch window; confirmed by a follow-up window scan; `shots/deploy/hub_deploy_final.png`). **Device-verified on the itel (091945939L000716):** Add Application→CSWeb→Update (LoginApp "New version available") → logged in `se-001` → enumerator menu now shows **"+ Receive Assigned Data"** (no "(Patient)"); firing Receive with no host → cancel surfaced the **new BT-on failure message verbatim** (`itel_58_enum_menu.png`, `itel_60_btmsg.png`). **Send→Collect Bluetooth leg re-verified (2-tablet, observable):** conducted a fresh F3 partial-case `010280001502` on the A23 (reused the known-good `010280` PSGC prefix so the case-key gate accepted it), then supervisor `fs-01` **Collect Interviews from Enumerators** (Bluetooth host) ← enumerator **Send My Interviews to Supervisor** → both confirmation messages fired and **the itel's F3 count moved 1→2** (received `…502`; A23 keeps both = non-destructive). This + the earlier Assign→Receive means **both Bluetooth halves are re-confirmed on the current build** (Relay was device-confirmed 06-28). Two test cases (`010280001501/502`, old-EA test prefix) now sit on both PatientSurvey apps — harmless test data, cleared by any remove+re-add. Logged out clean. No git (Carl handles). ## 2026-06-21 (CSWeb Map Report v1 — pin map, built + deployed + verified)
**Brainstormed Map Report features → confirmed GPS path → spec'd → built v1.** Carl asked what features a CSWeb Map Report should have; the answer hinged on whether we capture **point GPS or only area codes**. Checked the CSPro source: **all 3 instruments auto-fetch real device GPS** on focus then protect it — F1 `FACILITY_GPS_*`, F3 `FACILITY_GPS_*` **and** `P_HOME_GPS_*` (two points), F4 `LATITUDE/LONGITUDE`+`HH_GPS_*`. Then confirmed **on the box** the coords flow into the breakout DBs (dedicated tables keyed on `level-1-id`: F1/F3 `rec_facility_capture`, F3 `rec_patient_home_capture`, F4 `household_geo_id`) with real data present (F1 3/4, F3 7/8 ×2, F4 4/5 — the ~1 blank/instrument = desktop/no-radio). Storage gotchas: coords are **`varchar(12)`** (cast to decimal); `questionnaire_number` **drops its leading zero** (11 chars) so `LPAD(...,12,'0')` before deriving the 9-digit facility code. Wrote **`deliverables/CSWeb/CSWeb-Map-Report-Spec.md`** (v1 pin map / v2 F3 dual-point + spatial-QA / out-of-scope choropleth+coverage-target).
**Built v1: `deliverables/CSWeb/csweb-map-gen.py`** — sibling of the dashboard gen (runs on the box, host python3 + `docker compose exec`), one marker per case (F1 facility, F3 facility, F4 household) colored by Case Status, **Leaflet + markercluster** (both **vendored** at `/docs/assets/`, no CDN JS), OSM tiles. Same filter bar as the dashboard (**Instrument · Region · Status · Visit-from · Visit-to · Reset**), client-side. **No-fix transparency:** cases with no usable GPS counted in a visible badge, not plotted; **PH-bounds (lat 4.5–21.5, lon 116–127) validation** drops garbage coords into a separate "bad" tally. Same XSS-safe `<script type="application/json">`+`JSON.parse` payload pattern as the dashboard. Facility labels via `csweb_reports.facility_names` (code9 from padded QN). Writes **`/docs/map.html`**.
**Deployed + cron + verified.** scp'd gen → `/opt/`, ran it (**points=13** [F1 3/F3 7/F4 3], nofix 1/1/1, bad 0/0/0, totals 4/8/4). Installed cron `*/15 * * * * cd /opt/app && python3 /opt/csweb-map-gen.py >> /var/log/csweb-map.log 2>&1` (alongside dashboard/monitor). **Browser-verified (chrome-devtools):** Leaflet+markercluster loaded, 13 plotted + "3 cases not plotted (no GPS fix)" badge, 32 OSM tiles, markers clustered (test data tight around Los Baños/UPLB); filter reconciliation exact (Status=Partial→4, F3+Completed→6, singular "1 case" grammar, Reset→13); **console clean (0 errors)**. **Cross-linked:** `help.html` guide-list ("Map Report — synced cases on a map"; `help.html.bak-2026-06-21`), dashboard footer → map, map footer → dashboard; all 3 links live (HTTP 200). See `CSWeb-Map-Report-Spec.md`, [[project_aspsi_supervisor_app]].
**v2 (same day) — F3 dual-point + spatial QA.** Rewrote `csweb-map-gen.py` to a per-source model: F3 now emits TWO layers — facility AND **patient-home** (`rec_patient_home_capture.p_home_gps_*`) — as toggleable layers (◆ diamond = home) with an optional **connector line** per case. Added a **Data quality** filter (All / Flagged / Low accuracy / Duplicate location) and field-QA flags (red ring on flagged markers): **low accuracy** (`accuracy > 50 m` OR `satellites < 4`) and **duplicate location** (identical coords to ~1 m shared by >1 distinct case) — the latter computed **only for per-respondent layers (F3 home, F4 household)**, never facility points (many patients legitimately share one facility's GPS → would false-alarm). **DATA-QUALITY CORRECTION (root-caused, not papered over):** first v2 run flagged ALL 20 points low-accuracy — because the breakout stores `satellites = 0` on every record (test devices/emulator don't populate `gps(satellites)`); accuracy is actually fine (10–20 m). A real fix can't have 0 sats, so the generator now treats `sat ≤ 0` as *unreported* → lowAcc back to 0 (honest). **Deployed + browser-verified (chrome-devtools):** points=20 (f1 3 / f3 facility 7 / f3 home 7 / f4 3), dupLoc=0, lowAcc=0; toggling patient-home off → 13, F3-only → 14, **6 connector lines** (6 F3 cases have both points), Flagged-only → 0, Reset → 20; console clean (0 errors). help.html blurb refreshed for the v2 capabilities (`help.html.bak-2026-06-21`).
**v3 (same day) — full A+B+C displacement / wrong-area QA (Carl chose the full tier via AskUserQuestion).** **Join discovery:** the breakout geo CODES are **survey-internal, not PSGC** (Ilocos Norte = region_code 1 / province_huc_code 2 / city_municipality_code 800), so area matching is **by NAME** — region names are word-reordered ("Region I (Ilocos Region)" vs the boundary set's "Ilocos Region (Region I)") → matched on a sorted-token key; province names match exactly. **Boundaries:** GADM's host is unreachable from the box but GitHub raw is; new `gen-ph-boundaries.py` curls **faeldon/philippines-json-maps 2011 lowres** (regions.json + 17 per-region province files), computes shoelace centroids + max-radius + simplified rings, writes `/docs/assets/ph-areas.json` (**17 regions, 80 provinces, 59 KB**; Batanes + Camiguin have null geometry → fall back to region level, logged). Run-once asset; the 15-min cron just reads it. **shapely isn't on the box → pure-Python ray-cast point-in-polygon.** **Tiers added to `csweb-map-gen.py`:** A = self-referential (F3 home > 50 km from its facility; facility point > 5 km off the centroid of its ≥3-case facility cluster); B = point beyond its **declared** province's own radius from that province centroid; C = PIP that asserts "wrong area" **only on positive containment in a DIFFERENT province** (robust to lowres border imprecision; falls back to region if province name unmatched). New `Data quality` filter options (Displacement, Wrong area) + warn chips + popup detail (km + the province it actually falls in). **Browser-verified (chrome-devtools):** points=20, QA low=0 dup=0 clusterOut=4 farCentroid=20 outArea=20, unmatched=[]; a sample flagged point reads **declared "Ilocos Norte", coordinates in "Laguna", 445.9 km from declared centroid** (all 20 contained in Laguna — the test cases were captured in Laguna but coded Ilocos Norte, the exact curbstoning signal); Wrong-area filter → 20, Displacement → 4, Reset → 20; console clean. help.html blurb + spec (now v1+v2+v3 BUILT) updated. Backlog: city/municipality-level checks (city_name unreliable now), newer PSGC vintage if recent-split provinces appear.
**Tester guides relocated to Laguna (so the next round produces NON-displaced cases).** The R4 wrong-area flags were a test-data artifact — testers captured GPS in Laguna but the guide's test prefix `010280001` codes the facility as **Ilocos Norte**. Carl's call (data-integrity option): point the guides at a **real DOH-sampled Laguna facility** rather than inventing Los Baños entries — **`040340002` = CABUYAO CITY HOSPITAL** (Region IV-A / Laguna; already in the facility list + `csweb_reports.facility_names`, so the map/dashboard label it). Updated all three tester guides (`docs/F{1,3,4}-CSEntry-Tester-Install-and-Test-Guide-2026-06.md`) + the monitoring companion: prefix `010280001`→`040340002` (13 refs each guide, 6 companion), line-41 location wording → "Region IV-A / Laguna · Cabuyao City Hospital", and a new §3 📍 note telling testers to **capture GPS where they are (Los Baños/Laguna) and stay co-located** so both the wrong-area (province) and displacement (facility-cluster >5 km) checks read clean. Verified: 0 old-prefix / 0 Ilocos refs remain; case-block ranges (F1 1xx / F3 5xx / F4 6xx) unchanged. **Docs-only — no app rebuild, no facility-list change** (province-level check + co-location is sufficient; the wrong-area detector left untouched). Round metadata (still R4-stamped, dates 06-12..15) left for Carl's announcement of the next round. No memory change.

Added an inline SVG favicon (kills the only console 404). **Verified in a real browser (chrome-devtools):** page renders, all charts draw, console clean (0 errors), and a live filter test (Instrument=F3 + Region=(unknown)) correctly hid F1/F4 and dropped the F3 count 8→1 with charts recomputing. The `csweb_reports` views remain for direct SQL/BI; the dashboard now reads per-case rows directly. (Note: test cases gained real field values during the session, so charts now show Public/Outpatient/Male-Female, not just blanks.)
**(8) Visit-date range filter.** Added each case's visit date (`field_control.date_first_visited`, YYYYMMDD, in the breakout — no cross-DB join) to the per-case rows; filter bar now has **Visit From / Visit To** date inputs (bounded to the data's min/max via the inputs' min/max attrs), folded into the same client-side filter (combines with Instrument + Region; Reset clears all; blank-date cases drop out when a range is set). **Browser-verified:** inputs bounded 2026-06-11→06-17; filtering to visit-date 2026-06-12 narrowed F1 4→2 (the two cases visited that day) with charts recomputing; console clean. (Chose visit date over sync timestamp — fieldwork-meaningful and already in the breakout; sync date would need a join to the main store's modified_time.)
**(9) Case Status (Completed / Partial).** Added a per-case `status` derived from `cases.partial_save_mode` (NULL/'' = Completed; set, e.g. 'add' = Partial) — a **Case Status doughnut** leads each instrument section, plus a **Status filter** (All / Completed / Partial) folded into the client-side filter. Browser-verified: status charts match the DB (F1 Partial 3/Completed 1, F3 Completed 7/Partial 1, F4 Completed 4); Status=Partial → cards F1 3 / F3 1 / F4 0; console clean. Dashboard filter bar is now Instrument · Region · Status · Visit-from · Visit-to · Reset. **Security:** automated review flagged XSS-via-JSON-in-`<script>`; fixed by embedding the payload in a non-executable `<script type="application/json">` block (HTML-escaped via `html.escape`) and reading it with `JSON.parse(el.textContent)` — `&,<,>` can't break out of the tag; verified a hostile `</script>` value becomes `&lt;/script&gt;` and round-trips. (First attempt used `\uXXXX` replace strings but the backslashes got mangled through the tooling to a no-op; the application/json approach avoids backslash-escaping entirely.)

## 2026-06-12 (Capture-type check swept to F1+F3; F1 date-format fix SHIPPED after a packager-gate discovery)
Carl: "apply the same capture-type check to F1 and F3" → optimizer dry-runs: **F3 CLEAN (0 changes)**; **F1: 2 date fields** (`DATE_FIRST_VISITED/FINAL_VISIT_TO_THE_FACILITY`) carried legacy hand-style `DataCaptureType=Date` + separate `CaptureDateFormat=YYYYMMDD`. First fix attempt (rewrite to combined `Date,YYYYMMDD`) **passed open+compile but FAILED the Deploy packager** — "Incorrect [Field] attribute: CaptureDateFormat" at "Creating package..." — the legacy line remained alongside the combined form. **LESSON (folded into cspro-compile-validate + cspro-auto-deploy skills): the publish packager is a STRICTER FMF parser than Designer open+compile; a packaging error aborts BEFORE upload, CSWeb untouched.** Root-cause fixes: `optimize_capture_types.py` strips legacy `CaptureDateFormat=` lines when the combined form is present; `cspro_compile_driver.py` runs the optimizer for ALL instruments (not just the F3/F4 bind branch), connects by launched PID (F1 spawns ambiguous/transient CSPro-titled windows → ElementAmbiguousError/InvalidWindowHandle on title match), and `clear_known_dialogs` survives F1's self-closing startup dialogs. Also learned: **`CSDeploy.exe` is its own process** (survives `taskkill CSPro.exe`, holds FMF locks → PermissionError on rewrite) and a publish can leave a stray headless `CSEntry.exe` — kill both on stale locks. F1 re-validated (block invariant OK incl. the Section C 259-block plan, verify 672/672 PASS, fresh-Designer "Compile Successful at 18:37:03") → agent-driven publish (`%f{UP 12}{ENTER}`, Carl's "ship now") → `auto_deploy.py F1 --deploy` → **"deploy succeeded"**, popup dismissed, dialog parked → patch note #f1-uat (p1781262483506949). The shipped F1 carries the Section C skip fix + the date fix. **Cross-checked the parallel workstream's staged F3/F4 skip-fix builds: their regen ran through the fixed pipeline — capture types intact (F3 22 DropDown+CheckBox+2 Date; F4 21+2); optimizer dry-run = 0 across F1/F3/F4.** All three instruments now carry the identical canonical capture-type style.

## 2026-06-12 (F3+F4 skip-aware screen-plan fix — STAGED, deploy pending Carl's go)
Ported F1's skip-boundary screen-plan fix (the [[project_capi_uat_triage_loop]] root cause) into F3 and F4 `generate_fmf.py`, pre-empting the same latent combined-screen bug class before testers hit it there. **Both generators already isolated gated other-specify text (`_is_gated_text`) but did NOT break screens at skip boundaries** — so a skip SOURCE could share a DisplayTogether screen with the field it skips over, and a skip TARGET could be pre-rendered on the screen the interview jumps into (identical to F1 #371/#375). **Fix (mirrors F1 inject_blocks):** added `parse_apc()` (reads the instrument `.ent.apc` → skip_sources = PROCs with `skip to`, skip_targets = `skip to <FIELD>` names, noinput_gated = PROCs with `noinput`), threaded into `derive_block_plan`'s SIMPLE-chunk loop only: `if chunk and nn in targets: break` (target starts a screen) + after-append `if nn in sources: break` (source ends a screen); `_is_gated_text` now also honors the noinput set. **F4's amount-matrix chunking (`is_matrix`/`_RUN_BLOCK_CAP=22`) + roster occurrence flow left untouched.** Canonical pipeline (`cspro_compile_driver build_instrument`) already runs dcf→apc→fmf, so apc-before-fmf is guaranteed. **FMF-only:** `.dcf`/`.ent.apc` byte-identical to the deployed patch-train state. **Verified:** verify_questions F3 787/787 PASS + F4 625/625 PASS (0 dead, 0 bad-skip); fmf_block_check OK; **Designer compile F3 Successful 18:37:55, F4 Successful 18:42:03**; F3 217→237 screens (max Pos 204 ≤228), F4 159→225 screens (max Pos 193). **DEPLOY BLOCKED at the publish click** — the production-deploy permission guard (correctly) requires Carl's explicit per-round go for a live CSWeb publish; the AskUserQuestion "Both" selection authorized the work but the production write is gated separately. Both builds staged + compiled, one approval from shipping. **Also closed a stale pre-opened F4 (HouseholdSurvey) deploy dialog** Carl had parked — it predated this fix so it would have shipped the OLD build; a fresh publish is required regardless. Files changed: `F3/generate_fmf.py` + `F4/generate_fmf.py` (+ regenerated `.fmf`/`.generated.fmf`). Video-reading packaged as standalone `read-video` skill (Carl's ask). See [[project_aspsi_capi_uat_r4]].

First five F1 R4 tester findings (#371–#375, AWSP, Section C smoke test) all triaged to ONE root cause: not the `.apc` skip logic (correct) but the R4 combined-view screen plan in `FacilityHeadSurvey.fmf` — questions, their Other-specify boxes, and skip targets shared 5-field DisplayTogether screens, and CSEntry renders every field on a shared screen regardless of skip/`noinput` gating. Symptoms: Q10→Q11 no-skip, Q12 specify over-trigger, Q13→Q14 no-skip (#371); Q19/Q21 cross-visible specify (#372); Q23 (#373); Q25 (#374); Q27 cross-visible + Q28 not skipped (#375). **Fix:** new maintained `F1/inject_blocks.py` (replaces the lost one-off `tmp/inject_f1_blocks.py`) — skip-source/target-aware chunking; every gated specify box gets its own suppressible screen; skip targets start fresh screens. 105→259 blocks, FMF-only (`.apc`/`.dcf` byte-identical). **Verified:** `verify_questions.py` F1 PASS (672/672 reachable, 0 dead-cond, 0 bad-skip), CSPro Designer compile **Successful** (driver + screenshot). **DEPLOYED to CSWeb:** fresh Designer ▸ File ▸ Publish and Deploy → `auto_deploy.py F1 --deploy` (package `FacilityHeadSurvey` + 8 PSGC files, main dict sync only, target `csweb.asiansocial.org/csweb/api`) → **"Application Deployed Successfully."** **Notified:** per-issue "fixed + retest" threaded replies under each bot notification in #capi-scrum, plus a consolidated F1 patch note to #f1-uat (update via CSEntry ⋮ → Update Installed Applications, fresh case). GH #371–#375 stay OPEN pending AWSP on-device retest confirmation; deploy confirmed on tracking #368. **Latent same-class bug** still in F3/F4 `generate_fmf.py` derivers (not yet ported). Triage loop [[project_capi_uat_triage_loop]]; round [[project_aspsi_capi_uat_r4]].
