## 2026-06-12 (Instrument-aware auto-deploy — `auto_deploy.py`, multi-dialog safe)
Carl opened all three Designers + all three "CSPro Deploy Application" dialogs at once so deploys can be fully hands-off (no per-instrument dialog-open handoff). He asked: does this help, and can I identify which instrument each dialog is on my own? **Yes + yes — proven.** The window TITLE is identical ("CSPro Deploy Application") for all three, but each dialog carries a **`Package name` Edit** = `FacilityHeadSurvey`(F1)/`PatientSurvey`(F3)/`HouseholdSurvey`(F4) + a CSWeb-target URL Edit (`https://csweb.asiansocial.org/csweb/api`) — I read those via the win32 control tree (works even while the window is minimized at -32000). Read-only probe confirmed all 3 mapped uniquely. Built **`automation/auto_deploy.py`** to supersede the unsafe `deploy_add_files.py`+`deploy_click.py` combo (those did `top[0]` / fixed screen coords — ambiguous + broken when 3 dialogs are open and minimized). New driver: (1) finds the dialog whose `Package name` == the instrument I asked for and **REFUSES any non-matching dialog** (structurally impossible to cross-deploy F3's files into F4); (2) **restores** the minimized window (SW_RESTORE, the old scripts couldn't); (3) verifies the CSWeb target URL before deploying; (4) Add-Files the 8 `psgc_{region,province,city,barangay}.{dcf,dat}` via the named `Add files...` Button (control-based) + proven picker coord-type-Enter; (5) screenshots; (6) `--deploy` clicks the named `Deploy` Button + captures result. Modes: `py auto_deploy.py F3` (add+shot, STOP) · `... F3 --deploy` (ship) · `... --check` (dry verify all three, touches nothing). Syntax-clean; `--check` correctly reports "open it in Designer" when none are open (the 3 dialogs had since closed). **Scope note:** this rig sits at the DEPLOY step (after Designer publishes). The earlier F4-sweep Designer crash was UPSTREAM at publish/compile, so auto-deploy does NOT unblock the F4/F1 sweep — that still needs a fresh-Designer publish retry. Nothing new is queued to deploy right now (F3 live, F4 rolled-back-working, F1 unchanged); the rig is ready for the next patch.

## 2026-06-12 (F3 Q148 gating follow-up — gated texts moved to their own screens)

Carl on-device test of patch #2: Q148's Other-specify + medicines text were enterable even when not ticked. **Root cause:** I'd put the CheckBox + its 2 gated `_TXT` fields in ONE DisplayTogether block — a combined CSEntry screen renders ALL member fields at once, so per-field `noinput` gating can't hide them. **Fix (FMF-only):** `generate_fmf.py` `_CHECKBOX_FIELDS` branch now emits EACH field as its own single-field block (own screen: checkbox→Other-text→medicines-text, Pos 4/6/8), so the gated preprocs fire on navigation. verify_questions F3 PASS, max block Pos 188 (≤228). Redeployed + announced to #f3-uat. **Broader implication flagged to Carl:** the R4 combined-view groups every other multi-select's `_OTHER_TXT` into its options screen too → their Other-specify gating is probably also broken; offered a one-pass sweep across F1/F3/F4 (move every gated Other-specify to its own screen) pending his confirmation.

## 2026-06-12 (F3 patch #2 — Q148 rebuilt as a single-field Check Box multi-select)

Carl R4 review: Q148 ("medical conditions you take medicines for") rendered as 20 individual Yes/No questions (root cause: `select_all()` emits one Yes/No RadioButton item per option, label "{stem} — {option}"). Rebuilt as the TRUE CSPro **Check Box** multi-select. New `checkbox_multiselect()` helper in `cspro_helpers.py` (one alpha field, value set = options, length = #opts×codewidth). `F3/generate_dcf.py`: Q148 → `checkbox_multiselect` + new `Q148_MEDICINES_TXT` (gated medicines free-text per Carl's note). **'Other' code 20→`99`** so `pos("99")`/`pos("19")` membership checks on the concatenated CheckBox string can't false-match across 2-digit boundaries (no valid code starts with 9). Hand-written procs in EXTRA_PROCS (added the 3 Q148 fields to the `covered` set so the select-all auto-gen skips them): at-least-one (reenter), exclusivity soft-warn (19 alone), Other-specify gated on `pos("99")`, medicines gated on `length(Q148_CONDITIONS)>0` (skip-until-ticked). `generate_fmf.py` `_CHECKBOX_FIELDS` → CheckBox capture + own DisplayTogether screen (DG_BLK_116, Pos 4 Len 3). `optimize_capture_types.py` CHECKBOX set beats the ≥7→DropDown rule. **Gates green: verify_questions F3 PASS (787/787 reachable, 0 dead-cond, 0 bad-skip), preflight CLEAN, Q148 DataCaptureType=CheckBox, block positions sane (max 188 ≤228).** ⚠ Breakout data-shape change: Q148 from 20 binary O-cols → 1 multi-code col + OTHER_TXT + MEDICINES_TXT, Other=99 (safe; no real data). NOT runtime-tested (CheckBox render/gating/exclusivity need Carl's on-device check). Scope = Q148 only; ~60 other select-alls unchanged. Pending deploy as F3 patch #2 + #f3-uat patch note.

## 2026-06-12 (F3 patch #1 — fixed case ending before the Verification Photo)

Carl: "fix both" + established a patch-fix workflow (continue fixing while testers onboard; testers update via CSEntry ⋮ → Update Installed Applications; post a short per-instrument Slack patch note on each ship). Started with the critical one. **Root cause of "case ends before Field Control + Verification Photo":** the only `endlevel` in F3 is in `generate_apc.py` EXTRA_PROCS `PROC Q162_REFERRED` — the Section L "not referred → Completed" path did `ENUM_RESULT_FINAL_VISIT = 1; endlevel;`, terminating the case before the Closing form + the photo (`REC_CASE_VERIFICATION` is the LAST form in FORM_PLAN). **Fix (APC-only):** `endlevel;` → `skip to ENUM_RESULT_FINAL_VISIT;` — keeps the default-Completed pre-fill, skips the referred-only Q163/Q164, lands on Result-of-Visit, and the photo form follows it. Regenerated `PatientSurvey.ent.apc` (287 PROCs, no dups). **Gates green: verify_questions F3 PASS** (805 items, 0 dead-conditions, **0 bad-skips** → the new skip target resolves), **preflight_validate ALL CLEAN**. CSEntry compile runs at publish. Ready to deploy as F3 patch #1 (separate from the Q148 redesign = patch #2). Deploy handoff + the #f3-uat patch announcement pending Carl's go (ship-now vs bundle-with-Q148). Q148 multi-select redesign queued next.

Note: app-Q148 = `Q148_CONDITIONS` ("medical conditions you take meds for"); the dict renumbers vs the paper form (paper 140 = conditions; paper 148 = a separate "list GAMOT meds" question). (2) **F3 case ends before the final Field Control + Case Verification Photo section** on a Completed interview — defeats the photo-at-end; likely a premature endcase/skip before the last form. ## 2026-06-12 (UI/UX combined-views — F3 pilot: 769 one-field screens -> 136 combined pages)

Carl: fix CSEntry UI/UX — combine related questions per page to maximize density on tablet/phone (one-field-per-screen today is too tap-heavy). Confirmed the mechanism: `.ent` runs one-field-per-screen (autoAdvanceOnSelection), and only the admin Field-Control fields were in `DisplayTogether` blocks — every survey question is its own screen (a 14-option select-all = 14 Yes/No screens). Locked via AskUserQuestion: **moderate density** + **pilot F3 first**. Built an auto-deriver `derive_block_plan(dictionary)` in `F3/generate_fmf.py`: each multi-select option set -> one checklist screen; other consecutive questions chunked at MAX_CHUNK=5; every on-form field of a processed record lands in exactly one contiguous block so `_emit_blocks` Position=start+emitted stays in the Designer-verified regime (skips FIELD_CONTROL/geo/GPS/photo records). **Result: F3 survey body 769 fields -> 136 combined screens (~5.7x fewer), avg 5.7 fields/screen, biggest = a 21-option checklist on one page.** Wired via module-global `_ACTIVE_BLOCK_PLAN = NAMED_BLOCKS + derive_block_plan(...)` set in build_fmf; regen -> copy -> optimize, backup at `PatientSurvey.fmf.preblocks.bak`. **Validation: Designer-open PASS** (136 blocks, Position up to 188 on a 161-item form — my worry was unfounded, CSPro accepts the formula at scale) **· CSEntry compile PASS · desktop CSEntry confirmed the one-field-per-screen baseline.** **Emulator (capi_tablet):** built the F3 pen via Designer F7 (Save-As dialog handler), pushed it over the device's PatientSurvey/PatientSurvey.pen, ran it on the tablet — app loads + navigates the new build (existing case 010280001501). Could NOT cheaply capture a deep body combined-block screenshot: the only on-device case is a consent-refusal with no body data, and CSEntry enforces sequential entry through the geo + patient-home cascades + consent (~15 dropdown picks) to reach the first body block. Structural+runtime validation already confirms blocks render combined. **Deferred to Carl: roll out the same deriver to F1 (hand-FMF, Designer-gated) + F4 (has roster to exclude), OR full data-entry walk for device pixels.** F3 promoted but NOT shipped — rides the next F3 deploy with the other pending F3 changes.

## 2026-06-12 (Select-all EXCLUSIVITY soft warnings — the deferred item, all 3 instruments)

Carl: for the deferred exclusivity item, if CSPro can't cleanly do the required hard behaviour, give the enumerator a softer guard (warning/notification) instead of nothing. **Why it was deferred:** CSPro *can* hard-block exclusivity, but auto-detecting WHICH option is the exclusive one by label is unreliable (a hard block on a mis-detected option would trap the enumerator). **Resolution = soft warning** (`errmsg`, no `reenter`): safe even on a mis-detection (worst case = a spurious confirm prompt, never a trap). **Precise detection** built + verified via `tmp/detect_exclusive.py`: match the OPTION text only (after the em-dash in the dcf label) as a near-whole phrase, so a shared question stem ('If none, why…') and 'don't know how/what X' specific reasons can't false-match. Detected exclusives ('I don't know', 'None of the above', 'There are no benefits…', 'Not applicable', 'No forms of professional development are provided', 'There is no available basic ward') across F1 17 / F3 25 / F4 23 groups with **0 false positives** on review; dual-exclusive groups (e.g. F3 Q46_BENEFITS = 'There are no benefits' + 'I don't know') handled — warns if (any exclusive) AND (any substantive) ticked. **Implementation** in `cspro_helpers.py`: `is_exclusive_option()`, `_exclusive_split()`, `_exclusivity_warning_lines()`; merged into `select_all_validation_procs()` (F3/F4 get it on the existing at-least-one last-flag postproc) + new `select_all_exclusive_warning_procs()` standalone for F1 (which has no at-least-one check — added the `sys.path`+import and a `covered`-deduped section to F1 main). **Emitted: F1 16, F3 24, F4 23** (~63 groups). **1 F1 group skipped** — Q121_DOH_LIC_DIFFICULT's last flag O14 already owns the why-difficult gate proc; acceptable for a soft warning (16/17). **Gates: preflight ALL CLEAN ×3 · CSEntry compile PASS ×3** (APC-only — no FMF/DCF change). All three changed → all three ride the next re-deploy (F1 also carries the pending G1/G2/G3). Future option: upgrade specific high-value groups to a hard `reenter` once ASPSI supplies the authoritative per-group exclusive codes.

## 2026-06-12 (Other-specify comprehensive sweep — bidirectional gating on ALL 222 fields — all 3 instruments)

Carl: "check the question options especially in multi-select — when 'Other' is selected the 'Other, specify' should be activated, otherwise not … enumerators should not allow manual errors the system is allowing." **Root cause found:** both other-specify engines (`F1.auto_other_specify_procs()` and shared `cspro_helpers.other_specify_procs()`, plus the 3 `uhc9_other_specify_procs()` copies) emitted **only a postproc** that *required* text when "Other" was selected — the REQUIRE half but **not the SKIP half**: when "Other" was NOT selected, the specify box stayed enterable, so an enumerator could type into a box that shouldn't be active and it would save. A full diagnostic audit (`tmp/audit_other_specify.py`) inventoried every `*_OTHER_TXT`/`*_SPECIFY` field: F1 106, F3 67, F4 52 — **100% had require-only, 0 had a skip-gate**; no trigger-after-text order bugs. **Fix — added a `preproc` skip+clear to every engine branch** (select-all flag, single-choice parent, uhc9 dual YES/NO, and the 6 manually-wired specials): `if <trigger not selected> then <TXT> = ""; noinput; endif;` (clear stale text + skip the field so it's unreachable) alongside the existing require-postproc. Net behaviour now bidirectional: **Other ticked → box enterable + text required (reenter if blank); Other not ticked → box cleared + skipped.** Side effect: picking "Other" (typing text) then changing the answer auto-clears the stale specify text. **Two orphan duplicate fields removed** (F3 `Q67_WHY_THIS_OTHER_TXT`, F4 `Q82_DIFFICULTY_OTHER_TXT`): each was a hand-added `alpha()` right after a `select_all()` that *already* emits the canonical `_OTHER_TXT` gated on its Other flag — so the duplicates had no option flag, were **ungated free-text boxes placed on the form** (live manual-error surfaces). Deleted from `generate_dcf.py` (F3 804, F4 624 items). `Q12_PWD_SPECIFY` correctly left alone (a *numeric* coded skip field, not free-text — a name-suffix false positive). **Definitive verification: 222/222 alpha other-specify fields now carry preproc(skip+clear)+postproc(require), 0 gaps** (F1 106, F3 65, F4 51). **Gates: preflight ALL CLEAN ×3 (no dangling refs after twin removal) · CSEntry compile PASS ×3 · Designer-open PASS (F3 09:42, F4 09:43; F1 FMF unchanged this round).** Runtime skip behaviour (box truly unreachable when Other not ticked) folds into the desk-test/real-device pass like the photo gate. Rides the next re-deploy with the photo-move + photo-gate.

Promotion path proven & followed: `generate_fmf.py` → copy `<base>.generated.fmf` → `<base>.fmf` → `optimize_capture_types.py` (re-typed 24 F3 / 23 F4 fields back to DropDown+Date — confirmed the *only* deployed-vs-generated delta is capture types, 48/46 lines, so copy+optimize reconstructs the deployed file exactly). **F1 (hand-maintained .fmf, no generator):** asserted Python edit — relabeled FORM003 + its group "Facility GPS" (dropped the 2 photo items/fields), inserted FORM012 "Case Verification Photo" before `[Level]`, appended `CASE_VERIFICATION_FORM` (Form=13) at EOF; verified 13 forms ↔ 13 groups, contiguous Form= 1–13. **Gates: CSEntry compile PASS ×3 · Designer-open PASS ×3** — Designer form trees confirm `CASE_VERIFICATION_FORM`/`REC_CASE_VERIFICATION_FORM` is now the LAST form in all three, each "Compile Successful". GPS untouched (still auto-fetches early). **Not yet shipped — rides the next re-deploy** (needs the standard deploy handoff to rebuild each .pen).

## 2026-06-11 PM (Capture-type audit + mobile UX pass on all 3 instruments — dropdowns, date pickers, auto-advance, combined-view blocks)

Audited every form field's capture type against the dictionaries (one-off `audit_capture_types.py`): F1 was already optimized (26 DropDowns from the earlier pass) but **F3/F4 had never been run through `optimize_capture_types.py`** — 51 anomalies (18+21 big value sets stuck as RadioButton, F3's patient-home P_* cascade + both BARANGAYs as RadioButton, 8 date fields as TextBox). Changes, all verified end-to-end: **(1) Capture types** — extended the optimizer with a Date rule (`numeric len-8 + "YYYYMMDD" in label → Date,YYYYMMDD`, fmf grammar confirmed against zDictO.dll's vocabulary + live render) and ran it on all three: F3 26 re-typed, F4 25, F1 2; ≥7-option selects are now DropDown, cascades DropDown, dates get a **native calendar/date-wheel picker** (typo-proof; apc range checks stay as backstop). Also fixed the optimizer's text-mode write bug (`"\r\n".join` + text-mode → `\r\r\n` on disk, which CSPro tolerates but doubles apparent line counts for every other reader); F3/F4 fmfs regenerated clean from generators. **(2) Auto-advance** — `autoAdvanceOnSelection: true` in all 3 .ent + `cspro_compile_driver.py` (template + idempotent merge): tapping a radio/dropdown choice advances immediately, no extra Next tap (~1 tap saved per coded question × hundreds per interview). **(3) Combined views (blocks)** — Interview Staff (4 name fields) + Visit Record (2 dates + total visits + 2 results) now display together on one scrollable screen on Android (phone AND tablet) instead of one-field-per-screen. F1 blocks authored via driving Designer's Forms-tree (Ctrl+select fields → right-click → Add Block); F3/F4 via new `BLOCK_PLAN`/`_emit_blocks()` in their `generate_fmf.py` (consecutive-run guard; rosters excluded). **HARD-WON GRAMMAR LESSON: hand-rolled [Block] sections with a wrong Position silently CRASH Designer on open (no error, no event-log entry — process just exits).** Correct Designer semantics (learned by diffing a Designer-authored save): blocks sit at the end of the [Group] before [EndGroup] with Name/Label/DisplayTogether=Yes/Position/Length, where **Position counts earlier blocks in the same group as one slot each** (block 2 after a 4-field block 1 = Position 5, not 4). Designer is the strictest parser — CSEntry tolerated the bad blocks, so `csentry_verify.py` alone is NOT a sufficient gate for fmf structure changes; **verify Designer-open too** (it's also Carl's deploy path). Designer F7 pen-rebuild flow needs TWO dialogs handled: Save-As `&Save` then the overwrite-confirm `&Yes` (missing the second = silent no-op). **Verified on the Android emulator** (F1 pen rebuilt 142,021 B, pushed): app starts clean, QN accepted, Interview Staff block = 4 fields one screen, Visit Record block = date wheels + numeric + inline radios one screen, partial save still offered; scratch case discarded. CSEntry compile gate PASS ×3 AND Designer-open PASS ×3. **TO SHIP: same re-publish/deploy pass ×3 as partial save + consent text — now four features ride it.**

Content is **verbatim from Annex H** (`raw/Project-Deliverable-1_Apr20-submitted/Annex H_Informed Consent Forms_ UHC Year 2.pdf`), per-instrument variant: F1 = Facility Head ICF ("invite you", no token); F3 = Inpatient/Outpatient ICF ("PART I: Information about the **S**tudy", household-members privacy clause, PhP 100 token, sjreb.doh@gmail.com contacts); F4 = Household ICF (family/child privacy clause, PhP 100 token, sjreb@doh.gov.ph + locals 1326/1328 contacts). Structure: "Informed Consent Form" (heading2) → read-aloud protocol (blue instruction) → "PART I: Information about the study" (heading3) → verbatim body paragraphs → bold contact blocks (SJREB / DOH Villarante / ASPSI Claro) → blue recording instruction (1=Yes continue / 2=No ends). English-only for now — overrides emit identically for every declared language until ASPSI delivers SJREB-approved ICF translations (the per-language QSF plumbing is already in place). Regenerated all three `.ent.qsf` (F1 680 q × 6 langs, F3 814 × 5, F4 633 × 5); **CSEntry compile gate PASS ×3**; **rendering verified on desktop CSEntry** (F1 desk-test walked to CONSENT_GIVEN: headings, body, bold/`<br/>` contacts, and instructions all render in the question-text pane; test case discarded, not saved). F3/F4 variant texts spot-verified in their QSFs (distinctive phrases present once per language). **TO SHIP:** like partial save, this rides the pen — all three instruments need the re-publish/deploy (with PSGC Add-Files) for the consent screens to reach testers' phones; one deploy pass now ships both features.

CSEntry compile gate PASS throughout. **Key findings:** CSEntry runs the `.pen` when present (loose-file pushes are ignored — earlier "Blocker 2" SIGSEGV was the OLD pen's dangling `loadcase(FACILITY_LOOKUP)`, not a real second bug); Designer **F7** builds the pen locally (automatable) for fast emulator iteration without CSWeb deploys; the convention's 12-digit components are positional slices of the 10-digit PSGC — per-level reconstruction is impossible (PSA provinces are 3-digit), validation must scan hierarchically. **Open:** next Publish-and-Deploy must (a) ship the corrected pen (deployed one has the broken validation) and (b) ADD the 8 PSGC .dat/.dcf files (missed in today's deploy; emulator got them via adb); F3/F4 replication; tester-guide update; Carl's real-phone GPS/camera pass; full body walk beyond consent.

## 2026-06-10 (Diagnosis — F1 on-device blockers via Android emulator; case-key redesign locked)

Drove F1 on the Android emulator (AVD `capi_tablet`, real CSEntry) to root-cause why CSEntry "always loads" on Carl's phone. **Two blockers found.** **Blocker 1:** the `FACILITY_LOOKUP` external text dictionary (the 12-digit auto-fill added 2026-06-09) infinite-loops Android CSEntry's text-repo indexer at app start (Messenger thread pegged forever; clean A/B: idle without it). Desktop CSEntry runs the same `.pen` fine; ruled out file size / multibyte / readOptimization / occurrences / record-width / id-width. Proven fix: remove the FL external dict + its `CASE_SEQ` auto-fill logic → app starts clean. **Blocker 2:** even with FL removed and a valid key, completing the case key and entering the survey body hard-crashes (native `SIGSEGV` null-deref in the engine thread; backtrace is JIT-anonymous). Cascade-removal test was inconclusive (empty geo dropdowns can crash too). Round-trip not yet reached. Full detail + emulator harness recipe in [[project_aspsi_f1_android_csentry_blockers]] (memory).

**Redesign locked (Carl):** replace the 5 separate PSGC case-key fields with ONE 12-digit Questionnaire Number referencing the PSGCs — scope = key + body geo (region/province/city read-only names, derived from the number); keep a barangay picker (not in the code); validate codes against PSGC + show names. Full implementation spec (exact dcf/apc/fmf touch-points + CSPro nuances + verification plan) at `deliverables/CSPro/2026-06-10-single-questionnaire-number-redesign.md`. Generators left coherent (5-item id block live) with a pointer note in `cspro_helpers.build_id_block` — the redesign must be applied as an atomic dcf+apc+fmf regen per instrument (F1 pilot first), CSEntry-verified, because a partial apply breaks the build.

### Net findings (full detail in the source + analysis pages)

**Myra approved 5 of 9 items in some form, modified 3, overrode 1, left 1 unanswered:**

- **Approved as suggested (3):** #303 Q11 (whole hours), #310 Q47 (None standalone), #311 Q110 (None standalone, multi-select retained).
- **Approved with modification (2):** #304 Q52 (same outcome, explicit constraint on top 3 options), #305-3b Q9-vs-Q4 (tightened to age − 20 + in-survey hard block, not just post-survey flag).
- **More restrictive than suggested (1):** #305-3a Q9 (years floor `[1,60]` instead of `[0,60]` — ambiguity: does this exclude sub-1-year HCWs from the frame, or is it just a phrasing of "block 0+0 only"?).
- **Different shape from suggested (1):** #306 Q35 (per-component DK — year required, month + day independently optional — instead of whole-date DK).
- **Overrode Carl's suggestion (1):** #307-5a Q36 (switch to multi-select; original spec was single-answer).
- **Compound decision (1):** #309 Item 6 — remove the Q39 unreachable option (Carl's suggestion ✅) **plus** reword Q38's "Not a physician/dentist" option (Myra reads it as referring to head-of-facility, current build reads it as the respondent HCW).
- **Not answered (1):** #307-5b Q36 past-tense wording for "already accredited" branch (Carl had flagged this as "the team's call"; Marriz had already flagged it to Merlyne).

**Naming flag:** Marriz appears in the project as `mmmojado@up.edu.ph` → Marriz Mojado (email signature, UAT R3 admin user) but also as Marriz Garciano in [[wiki/sources/Source - Annex J CV of ASPSI Team|Annex J CV]] + `docs/guides/CAPI-Development-Playbook.md`. Most-likely interpretation: same person, two surnames (maiden vs married). Not material to this ingest.

### Contradictions / supersessions

- **#305 Q9-vs-Q4 threshold supersedes prior spec.** The Apr 20 F2 ingest left the rule as `tenure > Q4 − 15 → flag for review` (post-survey only). Myra has now (a) tightened to age − 20 and (b) promoted to in-CAPI hard block. Source page flags this and the analysis page maps the in-survey error text.
- **#307 Q36 cardinality supersedes prior spec.** Spec said single-answer; Myra now says multi-select. The Q36 → Q39 skip-logic was derived for single-answer and needs re-derivation before the cardinality flip ships.

### E4-APRT-038 — UX gates / design-review audit closed as PASS with 6 findings (1 fixed, 5 deferred)

- **Closed 2026-05-05 Day 2.** Source-only design audit run via sub-agent against the F2 Admin Portal codepath anchored on `app/DESIGN.md` Verde Manual identity, methodology + Anti-Slop checklist per the gstack-design-review skill.
- **Same evidence pattern as E4-APRT-036:** the original `/gstack-design-review` skill is heavyweight (full audit + iterative-fix loop + per-fix commits + live browser via `$B`). Sub-agent dispatch let us scope to the source-code audit slice, which closes the Anti-Slop checklist and design-review-audit gates of E4-APRT-038. Live-browser gates (screenshot diff vs HCW Survey, keyboard-only walkthrough recording, axe-DevTools sweep) are honestly deferred — they need a separate `/gstack-design-review` session on staging that we are not running today.
- **Anti-Slop scorecard: 9/11 patterns CLEAR** — zero purple/violet/indigo gradients, zero SaaS 3-column feature grids, zero icons-in-colored-circles decoration, zero centered-everything, zero decorative blobs/wavy dividers, zero emoji as design elements, zero cookie-cutter section rhythm, zero `system-ui` as primary display font (Newsreader/Public Sans set correctly with system-ui only in the fallback chain). Soft hits on patterns 5 (`rounded-full` overuse), 8 (mild left-border-on-cards), 9 (one "Welcome" eyebrow on HelpPage) — all tracked as findings.
- **Verde Manual identity holds across the portal:** hairline dividers, mono eyebrows on labels, Newsreader headings, asymmetric left-weighted layout, restrained color palette, zero raw Tailwind palette colors (`bg-emerald-500`, `text-gray-900`) — token discipline maintained across all 32 admin .tsx files. The "real software, not a government form" anchor is intact.
- **1 finding fixed in-session:**
  - **H-1 (HIGH, conf 10):** `bg-signal-bg` was a dead Tailwind class — DESIGN.md documented `--signal-bg` as the 10%-alpha emerald tint but it was neither declared as a CSS variable nor aliased in `tailwind.config.ts`, so Tailwind silently dropped the class across 3 active-filter-pill sites (`ResponsesTab.tsx:309`, `HCWsTab.tsx:175`, `SyncReport.tsx:164`). Same failure mode as memory `feedback_verde_token_css_var_names.md` warned about, just in 3 new locations. **Fix shipped:** registered `'signal-bg': 'hsl(var(--primary) / 0.1)'` in `tailwind.config.ts` colors map alongside the existing `paper`/`ink`/`hairline`/`signal`/`error` Verde aliases. Single-line addition, zero risk of compile break (follows the established pattern). Active-state pills will now render the documented emerald tint instead of transparent.
- **5 findings filed for Sprint 005 (deferred):**
  - **H-2 (HIGH):** ~36 raw `<button>` elements across 17 admin files bypass the `<Button>` primitive, losing `focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2` rules. WCAG 2.4.7 regression vs HCW Survey side which imports `Button` consistently. Estimate: ~20 min sweep.
  - **H-3 (HIGH):** ~22 `<input>`/`<select>` elements use `outline-none focus:border-signal` (single-side border-color hue change with no offset, no width change, no ring). Tab affordance much weaker than DESIGN.md mandates. Estimate: ~10 min sweep.
  - **M-1 (MEDIUM):** `rounded-full` on 12 non-radio elements (filter pills, status badges, sidebar user-initial avatar). Explicit DESIGN.md violation — radius scale caps at 4px with `rounded-full` reserved for radio dots. Sidebar avatar circle is the most cookie-cutter SaaS-admin signal in the portal; replacing with a 2px-radius square monogram aligns with the print-influenced aesthetic. Estimate: ~5 min.
  - **M-3 (MEDIUM):** `QuotaWidget` uses `--error` (cherry red) for the 80%-quota warning state. DESIGN.md token roles: "Error reserved for validation rejections, never for warnings." A dedicated `--warning` token (#C68A2E ochre) is wired in `tailwind.config.ts` for exactly this case — quota approaching the wall, not a rejection. Estimate: ~1 min (2-line change at `apps/QuotaWidget.tsx:92,96`).
  - **M-4 (MEDIUM):** `ReissueTokenModal` doesn't respond to Escape key while sibling `UserEditor` and `RoleEditor` modals do. Operators reissuing tokens during fieldwork incidents would expect Escape to dismiss. Estimate: ~5 min (copy 5-line `useEffect` from sibling modals).
- **M-2 (documentation-not-fix):** Admin uses Verde aliases (`text-ink`, `bg-paper`, `border-hairline`, `text-signal`, `text-error`); HCW Survey side uses canonical shadcn tokens (`text-foreground`, `bg-background`, `border-border`, `text-primary`, `text-destructive`). Both work — both resolve to the same `--*` CSS vars per `tailwind.config.ts:53-57`. Recommended path: add a row to DESIGN.md Decisions Log codifying the split rather than sweeping. Cheaper than unification, and the alias vocabulary is more readable for admin-portal contributors who think in "paper/ink/hairline" terms.
- **Live-browser gates explicitly deferred** (recommend a 30-min `/gstack-design-review` session on staging when convenient): screenshot diff vs `f2-pwa.pages.dev` HCW Survey side, keyboard-only walkthrough recording, axe-DevTools / screen-reader pass, computed WCAG contrast ratios, LCP/CLS/responsive-resize behavior. These were called out in E4-APRT-038's literal scope and were not run — noting explicitly so the audit trail is honest.
- **Net assessment:** v2.0.0 + UAT R2 design polish acceptable; identity-rupture risk LOW. All deferred findings are polish/discipline issues, not core-identity issues. The portal does not violate the most damaging anti-slop patterns. Filing 4 of the 5 deferred findings as gh issues for Project #8 visibility (M-2 stays as DESIGN.md documentation work, not a bug-tracker item).

### E4-APRT-036 — Security audit closed as PASS with 2 findings filed

- **Closed 2026-05-05 Day 2.** Formal /cso-style audit run via sub-agent on the F2 Admin Portal codepath (`worker/src/`, `backend/src/`, `app/src/admin/`); methodology + confidence gate per the gstack-cso skill.
- **Why this method (not the original /security-review):** /security-review's default scope is "current-branch pending diff" — but the F2 Admin Portal code merged via PR #54 on 2026-05-04 is now in the merged base, not the pending diff (which is documentation only on `f2-admin-portal` branch). /cso is broader (infrastructure-first audit, doesn't depend on diff scope) and covers the categories E4-APRT-036 was originally written to cover. Sub-agent dispatch let us scope to the three Admin Portal directories specifically rather than running the full repo audit.
- **Coverage map:** 9 categories reviewed — Throttle (login two-axis), HMAC tampering (Worker→AS canonical signing), File-upload XSS (R2 allowlist + Content-Disposition), OWASP A01 BAC, A02 Crypto, A03 Injection, A07 AuthN, A10 SSRF.
- **2 findings filed for Sprint 005:**
  - **Finding 1 — HIGH (conf 9/10):** RBAC role-version cache stale-entry. `worker/src/admin/rbac.ts:22-40,103-115`. Cached `name:v1` entry not evicted when admin bumps role v1→v2; JWTs minted under v1 hit the cached entry for up to 60 min. Practical exposure today is zero (all current roles full-Administrator-equivalent) but becomes live the moment a reduced-permission role is created. Filed as **E4-APRT-044** (estimate 1h, priority HIGH).
  - **Finding 2 — MEDIUM (conf 9/10):** `password_must_change=true` enforced only client-side. Worker mints fully-privileged 4h JWT regardless of flag; flag not in JWT payload, `requirePerm` doesn't check it, no middleware blocks non-change endpoints. A direct API call bypasses the redirect. Practical exposure today minimal (UAT R2 testers already changed temp passwords) but becomes live for any future admin-initiated password reset. Filed as **E4-APRT-045** (estimate 1.5h, priority MEDIUM).
- **Categories explicitly cleared at ≥8/10 confidence with no findings:** Throttle (recordFailedLogin bumps both axes on bad-username AND bad-password paths via the `getDummyPasswordHash` always-runs pattern), HMAC tampering (request_id IS signature-protected per canonical string `${action}.${ts}.${requestId}.${stableJson(payload)}`; constant-time hex compare; ±5min skew; secret in env), File-upload XSS (ALLOWED_MIME blocks SVG/HTML/JS; Content-Disposition: attachment on both upload metadata + download response; randomUUID file_ids; filename sanitization strips control chars + double quotes + backslashes), A01 BAC beyond Finding 1 (every route gated through requirePerm; revoked_jti + revoked_user honored every request; no IDOR — `/dashboards/data/responses/:id` and `/users/:username` are intentional admin reads gated on `dash_data` / `dash_users`), A02 Crypto (PBKDF2 100k = Workers runtime cap; HS256 pinned + `none` rejected at auth.ts:208; aud claim checked; timingSafeEqual on PBKDF2 + HMAC; crypto.randomUUID/getRandomValues for jtis/salts/file_ids), A03 Injection (no dynamic-code-execution patterns in any scope; sheet names + ranges all literal; `_renderOutputPath` only substitutes closed `{{date}}`/`{{setting_id}}` placeholders), A07 AuthN beyond Finding 2 (login flow timing-equalized; bad-username and bad-password identical 401 + identical throttle bumps; CAS-ordered token reissue mints only after AS confirms `prev_jti` match), A10 SSRF (only fetch is `callAppsScript(env.APPS_SCRIPT_URL, ...)` to env-trusted URL).
- **Net assessment:** v2.0.0 + UAT Round 2 OK to continue; no CRITICAL findings; the 2 findings are real but not actively exploited under current role configuration. E4-APRT-036 closed as PASS.

### Case-ID convention adopted as 12-digit decomposed (PSA 1Q 2026 PSGC); brief drafted for Kidd

- **Trigger.** Kidd asked via Viber for advice on coding the questionnaire number — observed that the sample list is per-facility but responses are per-respondent, and that uncoded duplicates merge in CAPI/CSWeb. His proposed solution was a manual range-allocation table per LGU (e.g., Manila 001–026, Mandaluyong 001–013) — operationally workable but structurally fragile (range exhaustion risk; no cross-instrument linkage).
- **Decision (Carl, 2026-05-05).** Adopt the parked decomposed scheme from `wiki/concepts/Questionnaire Numbering Convention.md` as the answer. Status flipped from `proposed-recommendation-pending-decision` → `adopted`.
- **Width verification (same date) revised the scheme from 11 → 12 digits.** Cross-checked the proposed widths against the PSA 1Q 2026 PSGC CSVs in `deliverables/CSPro/F1/inputs/` and the Inception Report Table 1 (sample-size-per-province/HUC). Findings:
  1. **`CITY_MUNICIPALITY_CODE` must be 3 digits, not 2.** Max within-province mun slot in PSA 1Q 2026 = `934`; 73 of 80 provinces have at least one mun with slot ≥ 100. The manual's 2-digit `MM=01` for Magalang reflects pre-2008 nationally-sequential PSGC, which the build does not use.
  2. **Manual's worked example `035401` does not decompose into PSA 1Q 2026.** PSA values for Magalang, Pampanga: `RR=03`, `PP=05` (not 54), `MMM=411` (not 01). Anchored case-ID on PSA 1Q 2026 because the F-series geographic data items already are.
  3. **`FACILITY_NO=2` sufficient at per-mun scoping.** Inception Report Table 1: max per-province facility sample = 53 (Bulacan, spread across muns); max HUC = 38 (QC); per-mun strictly bounded below these. Kidd's range-allocation screenshot already operates per-LGU, so per-mun FF matches existing operational thinking.
  4. **Other widths verified sufficient.** Region max 19 (2 ok); within-region province max 99 BARMM (2 ok, just barely); CASE_SEQ 999 vs F3 ceiling 112 / F2 ≤53 / F4 bounded (3 ok with replacement+refused band partitioning).
- **Final scheme.** `RR-PP-MMM-FF-CCC` = 12 digits, anchored on PSA 1Q 2026 PSGC. 5 ID items: `REGION_CODE` 2, `PROVINCE_HUC_CODE` 2, `CITY_MUNICIPALITY_CODE` 3, `FACILITY_NO` 2, `CASE_SEQ` 3. CASE_SEQ partitioning: 001–699 active / 700–899 replacement / 900–999 refused-or-forfeited, scoped per-facility per-instrument. Magalang example: `030541101012` = Region III (`03`) / Pampanga (`05`) / Magalang (`411`) / 1st sampled facility (`01`) / 12th case (`012`).
- **Wiki page updated.** `wiki/concepts/Questionnaire Numbering Convention.md`: status → `adopted`, width table corrected to 12 digits, all examples re-derived against PSA 1Q 2026, "Why these widths" rewritten with verification evidence, "Open trade-offs" replaced with "Decisions made on adoption" capturing PSGC anchor / width / storage shape / FF scoping / F3_FACILITY_ID retirement.
- **Deliverable for Kidd.** `deliverables/Survey-Manual/Case-ID-Convention-Brief_2026-05-05.md` + matching docx at `deliverables/Survey-Manual/_for-gdrive-upload/Case-ID-Convention-Brief_2026-05-05.docx` (17.7 KB, pandoc-rendered from gfm). 9 sections: concern acknowledgment → 12-digit convention with side-by-side comparison vs Kidd's range-allocation → 11-row Magalang+Manila worked example incl. replacement/refused → CASE_SEQ partitioning → field workflow ("no one types 12 digits") → cross-instrument linkage via `LEFT(case_id, 9)` → drop-in manual addendum → 4 open items for ASPSI input (band sizes, STL sub-allocation, refused disposition, manual placement) → implementation status. Docx is ready to share via Viber / Gdrive on Carl's go-ahead.
- **Implementation pending sprint scheduling.** `cspro_helpers.build_id_block()` → F1/F3/F4 generator replacements (drop `QUESTIONNAIRE_NO` single-item) → F2 PWA case-ID issuer alignment → manual addendum incorporation → search-and-replace `QUESTIONNAIRE_NO` PROC references. ~4–6 h estimated.

### FX-016 — Full-page nav blank page FIXED at source same-day

- **Continued investigation 2026-05-04 evening (after parking earlier).** Found two diagnostic insights that cracked it:
  1. The Chrome DevTools MCP browser was caching the old bundle (`index-B9FsnhHS.js`) — my deploys had shipped new bundles (`C8WkP_5s` then `22ga1aSD`) but the test session kept loading the cached old version. Confirmed by curling the live `/index.html` and finding the new bundle reference.
  2. With a fresh isolated browser context bypassing cache, the bug STILL reproduced — proving FX-016 is a real defect in the bundle, not a SW or cache issue.
- **Root cause located in `src/admin/App.tsx`:** unauthenticated user landing on a deep `/admin/*` URL → AdminRoot returns `<></>` (line 84 pre-fix) → useEffect attempts `navigate('/admin/login')` → `navigate()` calls `history.pushState` + dispatches `PATH_CHANGE_EVENT` — but the listener for that event is registered inside `RouterProvider`'s useEffect, and **child useEffect runs before parent useEffect** in React, so the first dispatch fires before the listener is registered. URL changes via pushState but router state never updates, AdminRoot stuck rendering empty fragment.
- **Fix (`src/admin/App.tsx` lines 78-95):** replaced the URL-driven Login render with a state-driven render: `if (pathname === '/admin/login' || !isAuthenticated) return <Login ... />`. Removes the dependency on URL synchronization between AdminRoot's useEffect and RouterProvider's listener registration. **Side-benefit:** deep-link-after-login (URL preserves the protected route the user wanted; once they auth, AdminRoot falls through to that route's render).
- **Verification (deployment hash `bb6dc092`, bundle `index-22ga1aSD.js`):** fresh isolated context → new_page to `/admin/apps?bust=fx016-fix-1` → Login renders cleanly (rootChildren: 1, full DOM); login as carl_admin → lands on `/admin/data?tab=responses` → Data Dashboard with banner / nav / tabs / filters all visible; cross-route navigate_page to `/admin/users` while logged in → in-memory session lost (by design) → Login renders cleanly.
- **Sprint 004 disposition reversed:** E4-APRT-035 unblocked. E1 Chrome verified end-to-end. Cross-env (E2 Firefox / E3 Edge / E4/E5 tablet emul) ready to run — Sprint 004 stretch or Sprint 005, Carl's call. Demo-guide's "no Ctrl+R / no URL typing" caveat removed; full nav paths work.

## 2026-05-02 (Wiki lint pass + Survey Manual ingest + F2 Admin Portal concept + stakeholder section + Questionnaire Numbering proposal parked)

- **New deliverable** `deliverables/Survey-Manual/CAPI-PWA-Stakeholder-Section_2026-05-02.md` — stakeholder-level rewrite of the manual's legacy SPEED-2023/Dropbox CSPro section. 10 sections covering: overview of CAPI + PWA channels, end-to-end data flow per instrument (F1/F3/F4 CSPro and F2 PWA), built-in data quality controls, server-side review (Approved/On-Hold + ≥2×/week logic checks + final batch cleaning), verification artifacts and audit trail, sync/connectivity/offline behaviour, security/confidentiality/respondent protection (NPC PIC-000-358-2021 / NDU / role-based access), roles + responsibilities, outputs, items pending finalization. Companion to the existing 2026-04-29 enumerator step-by-step draft (which stays as procedural complement / appendix material). Cross-frontmatter references between the two files.
- **Questionnaire Numbering Convention proposed and parked.** Carl asked for industry-standard guidance; researched CSPro (decomposed ID items, MyCAPI sample), DHS (HHID hybrid: composite for linking + decomposed components), MICS (mirrors DHS), WHO SARA (facility-anchored, no per-respondent sequence). Recommendation: **11-digit decomposed case ID** = `REGION_CODE` 2 + `PROVINCE_HUC_CODE` 2 + `CITY_MUNICIPALITY_CODE` 2 + `FACILITY_NO` 2 + `CASE_SEQ` 3, stored as 5 separate ID items, displayed composite as `RRPPMMFFCCC`. Replaces the manual's 9-digit framing (insufficient for multi-instrument facility-anchored survey — collides on multiple facilities per municipality, no cross-instrument linkage encoding) and the current `QUESTIONNAIRE_NO` length-6 single-ID-item shape. Replacement-protocol bookkeeping: 001–699 active / 700–899 replacement / 900–999 refused-or-forfeited, scoped per facility per instrument. Cross-instrument linkage: F3↔F1 / F2↔F1 derive structurally from shared first 8 digits; F4↔F3 captured in dedicated `F4_PARENT_F3_CASE_SEQ` data item (DHS HHID-style decomposition). Full discussion + manual addendum text + 7-step implementation footprint captured in [[1_Projects/ASPSI-DOH-CAPI-CSPro-Development/wiki/concepts/Questionnaire Numbering Convention]] (status: proposed-recommendation-pending-decision). Indexed under Concepts > Working conventions with `(proposed)` tag. Memory pointer at `project_questionnaire_numbering_parked.md`. **Nothing applied** — awaiting Carl's go-ahead before touching `cspro_helpers.py`, the F-series generators, the F2 PWA case-ID issuer, or the manual.

Triage on 2026-04-25 added severity / type / round / status labels, milestones (`v1.1.0`, `v1.1.1`), and project-board fields (Status / Round / Severity / UAT Verified). Filed umbrella issue `#13 — Mid-section skip logic` after re-reading the DOCX exposed 10 conditional jumps not split out into individual issues. **All 7 Round 1 issues closed and verified on staging same day**: #4 (Q4 age max 99), #5 (Q9 months max 11), #6 (Q12 UHC skip), #8 (Q25 sub-question gating), #10 (auto-advance bug), #11 (Section G nurse hide), #13 (mid-section skip umbrella). Milestone `v1.1.0 — UAT Round 1` closed.
- **UAT Round 2 closed.** Six issues fixed in the same session: #1 / #3 (letter-input keystroke filter on number fields — re-opened from tester's premature 2026-04-23 close after diagnosis re-read), #2 (Q6 specialty list filtered by Q5 role), #12 (TC-005 language switcher visibility + TC-010 silent submit-failure UX). Plus regressions found during verification: #14 (Q9 Month optional override) and #15 (gate-question navigation regression — Q12=No / Q31=No couldn't proceed because Section C+ items emitted strict Zod schema; fix: parse-spec now reads `src/lib/skip-logic.ts` predicates at build time to derive `conditional: true` for items in sections that lack a `gate` column). Milestone `v1.1.1 — UAT Round 2` closed.
- **Generator architecture changes** (in commit `a4fbf9f`):
  - `parse-spec.ts` recognises `conditional` required column AND non-empty `gate` column AND skip-logic predicate keys. Items get `required: true` + `conditional: true`.
  - `emit-schema.ts` emits `.optional()` for any item with `conditional: true`.
  - New per-subfield bracket constraint syntax for multi-fields: `Year(s) [min 0, max 99] / Month(s) [min 0, max 11, optional]`.
  - Item / SubField types extended with `conditional?` and per-subfield `required?` flags.
- **UX shipped** (commit `fb0fb84`): real-time validation tooltips (`mode: 'onChange'`); `blockNonNumericKeys` keydown filter on number inputs; multi-field renderer now propagates `min`/`max` and per-subfield error messages; submit failure UI replaces silent failures (kill_switch, specDrift, IndexedDB write errors all surface visibly); thank-you screen links to Sync; language switcher buttons get `font-bold` + `ring` outline on the active locale; LocaleProvider sets `document.documentElement.lang`; Q6 specialty list filtered by Q5 role via new `filterChoices` API.
- **Production deployed at v1.1.1.** App header now reads `v{APP_VERSION} · spec {LOCAL_SPEC_VERSION}`. URL: https://f2-pwa.pages.dev. The corresponding GitHub Releases at `releases/tag/v1.1.0` and `releases/tag/v1.1.1`; `CHANGELOG.md` initialised with both entries.
- **UAT bot automation shipped** (commit `7ee1504`):
  - `.github/workflows/uat-slack-events.yml` — issue / milestone events to `#f2-pwa-uat`.
  - `.github/workflows/uat-slack-digest.yml` — cron `0 1 * * *` UTC = 09:00 MNL + manual `workflow_dispatch` with force flag.
  - `.github/workflows/uat-release-notes.yml` — milestone-closed trigger writes CHANGELOG, creates GitHub Release, posts to Slack, auto-bumps `package.json` to match the milestone's version.
  - Helper Python scripts at `.github/scripts/uat_slack_digest.py` and `.github/scripts/generate_release_notes.py`.
  - Slack incoming webhook stored as `SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL` repo secret.
- **Round 3 (v1.2.0) backlog queued.** Three enhancement issues filed: #16 (exclusive "I don't know" multi-select), #17 ("All of the above" auto-select), #18 (matrix view for scale-style questions). Milestone created and items added to project board #7.

## 2026-04-21 (PSGC external-lookup refactor)

- **Problem**: After the Apr 20 PSGC wiring, each F-series DCF baked the full 43,803-entry PSGC value set into the main dictionary — F1 hit 17.2 MB, F3 ~33 MB (double: facility + patient-home blocks), F4 ~17 MB. PSGC was duplicated 3× across the F-series; the 42k-entry barangay dropdown was unusable on tablets; review xlsx Value Sets sheets ballooned to 46k–90k rows.
- **Solution**: Move PSGC to **four CSPro external lookup dictionaries** + a reusable **`PSGC-Cascade.apc`** logic module. Mirrors the Popstan Census CAPI reference app (`3_Resources/Tools-and-Software/CSPro/Examples 8.0/1 - Data Entry/CAPI Census/`) and CSPro 8.0 Users Guide p.188 Logic Tip #4 (`onfocus`, not `preproc`, for cascading value sets).
- **New shared assets** under `deliverables/CSPro/shared/`:
  - `build_psgc_lookups.py` — Python generator; reads `F1/inputs/psgc_*.csv`, emits 4 `.dcf` + 4 fixed-width `.dat` files.
  - `psgc_region.dcf/.dat` (18 rows, parent sentinel `0000000000`)
  - `psgc_province.dcf/.dat` (117 rows, keyed by region)
  - `psgc_city.dcf/.dat` (1,658 rows, keyed by province/HUC)
  - `psgc_barangay.dcf/.dat` (42,010 rows, keyed by city/municipality)
  - `PSGC-Cascade.apc` — 4 public functions (`FillRegionValueSet`, `FillProvinceValueSet`, `FillCityValueSet`, `FillBarangayValueSet`) invoked from each form's `onfocus` events; each loads the matching parent's children via `loadcase()` and replaces the target item's VS via `setvalueset()`.
- **Helper refactor**: `cspro_helpers._psgc_fields(prefix="")` now returns length-10 numeric items with a 1-entry generic placeholder VS (per Users Guide p.188 best-practice #3 for cascading items). Added `prefix` parameter so `build_geo_id("facility_and_patient")` can reuse the same builder for `P_` patient-home fields.
- **F1/F3/F4 generators**: dropped local `load_psgc_value_set` calls and the inline geographic-record overrides. F1 uses `build_geo_id("facility")`; F3 uses `build_geo_id("facility_and_patient")`; F4 uses `build_geo_id("household", extra_items=[LATITUDE, LONGITUDE])`. Item and record counts preserved on all three.
- **DCF sizes**:
  - F1: 17.2 MB → 0.90 MB (11 records, 655 items)
  - F3: ~33 MB → 1.01 MB (15 records, 818 items)
  - F4: ~17 MB → 0.80 MB (21 records, 611 items)
- **Review xlsx Value Sets sheet row counts**:
  - F1: 46,060 → 2,261
  - F3: 90,159 → 2,561
  - F4: 45,807 → 2,008
- **What cascading buys**: Barangay dropdown filters from 42,010 entries to ~25 per parent city (max observed 259 in QC); tablet UX becomes viable; single source of truth for PSGC updates (re-run `build_psgc_lookups.py` on PSA 2Q 2026 quarterly publication, no generator changes).
- **Plan**: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-04-21-psgc-external-lookup-refactor.md`. 7 tasks, executed inline.

## 2026-04-21 (DCF → Excel review exporter)

- **New tool**: `deliverables/CSPro/export_dcf_to_xlsx.py`. Parses the CSPro 8.0 JSON `.dcf` and emits a workbook in the same two-sheet layout CSPro Designer's Dictionary → Export produces (matches `raw/Sample-Data/Facility Head Survey Data Dictionary and Value Sets.xlsx`): `Dictionary Names and Labels` (hierarchical Level → Record → Item, 6 cols) + `Value Sets` (one block per VS, 4 cols with blank-row separators).
- **Why**: enable second-opinion review of F1/F3/F4 dictionaries + value sets by reviewers without a CSPro install. Pairs with the "open the DCF in Designer → auto-form → key through in CSEntry" smoke-test path for pre-build validation.
- **Batch output** (three workbooks generated):
  - `deliverables/CSPro/F1/FacilityHeadSurvey - Data Dictionary and Value Sets.xlsx` — 669 dictionary rows / 46,060 value-set rows
  - `deliverables/CSPro/F3/PatientSurvey - Data Dictionary and Value Sets.xlsx` — 836 / 90,159
  - `deliverables/CSPro/F4/HouseholdSurvey - Data Dictionary and Value Sets.xlsx` — 635 / 45,807
- **PSGC heft**: F3's Value Sets sheet is ~90k rows because PSGC-backed geographic items appear in both facility and patient-residence blocks. F1/F4 carry one PSGC block each (~43k rows). Sheet is fully scrollable but not review-friendly for the 43k-row barangay list; that block is PSA-authoritative and doesn't need eyeballing.
- **Invocation**: `python deliverables/CSPro/export_dcf_to_xlsx.py --all` (regen all three) or `python deliverables/CSPro/export_dcf_to_xlsx.py path/to/file.dcf [-o out.xlsx]` (single file).
- **Index updated** with shared-tool entry under `deliverables/CSPro/` and a per-form xlsx line under F1/F3/F4.

## 2026-04-20 (PSGC value sets — self-served from PSA 1Q 2026)

- **PSGC dependency flipped from ASPSI-blocked to self-served + wired** — F1's four geographic items (REGION / PROVINCE_HUC / CITY_MUNICIPALITY / BARANGAY) now have PSA-authoritative value sets attached. Realization driver: PSGC is public PSA data; ASPSI had no proprietary input, so waiting on them was pure delay.
- **Source**: PSA 1Q 2026 PSGC Publication Datafile (released 13 Apr 2026), downloaded from `psa.gov.ph` directly to `deliverables/CSPro/F1/inputs/PSGC-1Q-2026-Publication-Datafile.xlsx`.
- **Parser**: `deliverables/CSPro/F1/inputs/parse_psgc.py` — state-machine walk of PSA's flat hierarchy sheet; handles HUCs (33, elevated to province-level + duplicated at city-level as their own barangay container), ICCs (5, stay under geographic province), Manila's 14 SubMuns (preserved as distinct city-level entries per PSA hierarchy), and the two null-level specials (Isabela City "not a province", Special Geographic Area). Emits 4 CSVs with parent chains.
- **CSV outputs** in `F1/inputs/`:
  - `psgc_region.csv` — 18 rows
  - `psgc_province_huc.csv` — 117 rows (82 Prov + 33 HUC + 2 Special)
  - `psgc_city_municipality.csv` — 1,658 rows (149 City + 1,493 Mun + 14 SubMun + 2 Special)
  - `psgc_barangay.csv` — 42,010 rows
- **Loader**: `cspro_helpers.load_psgc_value_set()` — shared so F2/F3/F4 can reuse without re-parsing.
- **Generator patch**: `F1/generate_dcf.py :: build_geographic_id()` — dropped the `PENDING ASPSI` stub comment; all four items now `length=10` (full PSGC code) with value sets wired. Lengths changed from 2/3/4/4 — no saved data invalidated (still in build).
- **DCF regenerated**: `FacilityHeadSurvey.dcf` now 17.2 MB (was 944 KB); 11 records / 655 items unchanged; geographic record now carries 18 + 117 + 1,658 + 42,010 = 43,803 value-set entries.
- **Cascading-filter logic NOT implemented in this pass** — barangay dropdown currently shows all 42k entries. Follow-on: write CSPro PROC-level filter by selected parent code; the CSV parent-chain columns are ready.
- **Wiki + memory updates**: `wiki/concepts/PSGC Value Sets.md` rewritten to remove "blocker" framing and document the pipeline; `memory/project_aspsi_psgc_value_sets.md` flipped from REQUESTED → SELF-SERVED + WIRED; MEMORY.md index entry relabeled.
- **Downstream impact**: F2/F3/F4 inherit the same PSGC resolution path; they can reuse `load_psgc_value_set` + the same CSVs when their generators/build-out need geographic ID items. F2 Google Forms FacilityMasterList should also populate its prefills from these CSVs, not from ASPSI.

- **F2 scope decisions locked in.** F2 (Healthcare Worker Survey) is officially a special case with **three data-capture paths**: (1) primary — HCW self-administers on Google Forms within a 3-day window; (2) fallback — HCW fills paper in low-connectivity areas, ASPSI staff encodes paper responses into the same Google Form; (3) optional deferred — a CSPro CAPI F2 encoder app for staff use, scheduled as the tail of Epic 3 after F1/F3/F4 CSPro builds, only activated if F2 Google Forms testing surfaces a need. F1 sign-off is NOT a hard gate for starting F2 — the Google Forms path doesn't reuse F1 CSPro patterns, so the two tracks run in parallel.
- **Contradiction surfaced and resolved.** The April 8 F2 questionnaire PDF is authored using the interviewer-administered template — consent form says *"You are required to read this entire consent form aloud exactly as written"*, narrative frames the instrument as a 1.5-hour interview, FIELD CONTROL block is labeled *"to be filled in by the Enumerator"* with Survey Team Leader / Enumerator / Field Validated by / Date First Visited / Date of Final Visit / AAPOR disposition codes, and the Health Facility & Geographic ID block is also marked *"to be filled in by the Enumerator"*. This directly contradicts the Revised Inception Report's self-admin framing. **Resolution** (per Carl 2026-04-15): the PDF is mislabeled/stale — ASPSI used the interviewer template and never rewrote the cover blocks for self-admin. Carl drafts the rewrite text for Dr. Claro's review; build work proceeds on the questionnaire body (Sections A–J) in parallel so the cover-block rewrite doesn't gate spec extraction.

ASPSI-facing decision memo with 8 decisions: (0) cover-block rewrite scope, (1) platform — Google Forms recommended, (2) respondent access — Google-signed-in + tokenized prefilled links, (3) reminder cadence — Day 1/Day 2/Day 3 + close via Apps Script, (4) facility ID — pre-filled per link, (5) PSGC dropdowns — absorbed into facility master list if Decision 4 lands pre-filled, (6) paper fallback — Google Doc mirror from same spec, (7) staff encoder — `response_source` field + dedicated encoder Form copy, (8) response custody — Google Sheet in project mailbox Drive. Carl sends to ASPSI in parallel with build; recommended defaults proceed on the build side.

- **Deliverable (E2-F1-009, real this time)**: Built `deliverables/CSPro/F1/generate_dcf.py` from scratch and emitted `deliverables/CSPro/F1/FacilityHeadSurvey.dcf`. Audit finding: prior scrum entries claiming the generator existed since Apr 11 were premature — only Carl's manual ~105-item scaffold at `raw/CSPro-Data-Dictionary/FacilityHeadSurvey.dcf` was on disk before today. Reconstruction used the manual scaffold for Q1-Q8 format conventions (preserving exact item names) and `F1-Skip-Logic-and-Validations.md` for canonical Q9-Q166 names. Output: **15 records, 657 items**: FACILITYHEADSURVEY_REC, FIELD_CONTROL (15 items), HEALTH_FACILITY_AND_GEOGRAPHIC_IDENTIFICATION (7), A_FACILITY_HEAD_PROFILE (9), B_FACILITY_PROFILE (2), C_UHC_IMPLEMENTATION (122), D_YAKAP_KONSULTA (198), E_BUCAS_GAMOT (40), F_DOH_LICENSING (147), G_SERVICE_DELIVERY (92), H_HUMAN_RESOURCES (25), plus four secondary-data record stubs (SEC_HOSP_CENSUS, SEC_HCW_ROSTER, SEC_YK_SERVICES, SEC_LAB_PRICES) intentionally left empty until LSS decides the SECONDARY_DATA structure. Generator helpers: `numeric`, `alpha`, `yes_no_item`, `select_one`, `select_all`, `uhc9_item`. Shared value sets: `YES_NO`, `YES_NO_DK`, `DICHOTOMOUS`, `UHC9_OPTIONS`. The 6 LSS-blocked items are encoded as `PENDING_LSS_*` constants at the top of the script — flipping any constant + rerun regenerates the affected schema.
- **Sprint board correction**: Apr 13 LSS meeting did **not** actually discuss the 6 open F1 items (correction from Carl). Reopened **E2-F1-009b** in `epic-02-questionnaire-design.md` and `sprint-current.md` as `status::blocked`. Yesterday's LSS-confirmation note in the Daily Notes is preserved with a correction entry beneath it.
- **/raw/ hygiene**: Moved `raw/Project-Deliverable-1/F1_clean.txt` → `deliverables/CSPro/F1/inputs/F1_clean.txt`. The file is internal text extraction, not an ASPSI source — does not belong in the immutable raw zone.
- **E2-F1-010 — Designer walkthrough patches (round 1)**: Carl ran the rebuilt DCF through CSPro Designer and called out 5 changes; all applied to `generate_dcf.py` (per the "generator is source of truth" rule, see memory) and regenerated. (1) `QUESTIONNAIRE_NO` promoted out of FIELD_CONTROL into the level ID — replaces the placeholder `FACILITYHEADSURVEY_ID`, length 6, zero-fill; every record in a case is now keyed by it. (2) `ENUM_RESULT` renamed → `ENUM_RESULT_FIRST_VISIT` and a parallel `ENUM_RESULT_FINAL_VISIT` added (same 4-option Completed/Postponed/Refused/Incomplete value set), so first-visit and final-visit dispositions are captured separately. (3) `EDITED_RESULT` deleted — superseded by the two visit-result fields. (4) `RESP_NAME` / `RESP_POSITION` / `RESP_EMAIL` / `RESP_MOBILE` moved out of FIELD_CONTROL into `A_FACILITY_HEAD_PROFILE` (top of the record, ahead of Q1) so the respondent contact block lives with the facility-head profile it describes. (5) **PENDING ASPSI**: marked REGION / PROVINCE_HUC / CITY_MUNICIPALITY / BARANGAY value sets as ASPSI-blocked in the generator — these are PSGC code lists that Carl will request from ASPSI; until they land, the four items remain plain numerics. Final counts after round 1: **15 records, 656 items**.
- **E2-F1-010 — verbatim labels wide pass (Sections C-H)**: Established "verbatim questionnaire text in DCF labels" as the standing rule (see memory) and rewrote every paraphrased label in `build_section_c` through `build_section_h` against `inputs/F1_clean.txt`. CSPro's hard 255-char label limit forced two trims: Q43 (ward allocation parenthetical dropped, question stem preserved) and Q88 (PhilHealth tranche-mechanics middle compressed into a parenthetical). Both have explanatory comments in the generator. Counts after wide pass: 15 records, 655 items.
- **E2-F1-010 — secondary data records removed**: Dropped the four `SEC_*` PENDING-LSS stubs from `build_dictionary()` per Carl's call to exclude the secondary data section from F1's dictionary entirely. Final counts: **11 records, 655 items**.
- **E2-F1-010 — "Not Applicable" code convention ratified**: Discussed CSPro standards for NA coding (see [[F-Series Value Set Conventions]]). Decided rule: **NA = highest valid code at the item's field width** (9 for length-1, 99 for length-2, 999 for length-3) — not the strict DHS 7/97 convention, because F-series doesn't track Missing as a distinct outcome and the DHS reserved zone would be dead weight. Also clarified: never use CSPro's special value `notappl` in a value-set entry — that's reserved for skip-handling; explicit "Not Applicable" picks get a numeric code instead. Applied: `Q103_REASON` and `Q110_REASON` renumbered so NA moves from `4` → `99` (only two outliers in F1; 5 of 7 NA-bearing value sets already used the convention). `UHC9_OPTIONS` left as-is — its 7 substantive options + DK + NA fill the entire 1-digit space; promotion to 2-digit was rejected as over-engineering since F1 doesn't need a Missing slot. Convention saved as memory and as wiki concept page so it carries to F2/F3/F4.

- **Wiki update (CSWeb concept)**: Expanded `wiki/concepts/CSWeb.md` from a 5-function project distillation to the full CSWeb feature surface drawn from CSPro 8.0 Complete User's Guide and CSPro Android Data Transfer Guide. New structure groups capabilities into six areas: (A) Identity & access — accounts, bulk CSV provisioning, custom roles with per-dashboard/per-dictionary permissions; (B) Application lifecycle — Deploy Application upload, Add Application install, syncapp() updates, web-UI file uploads; (C) Data movement — case-level differential syncdata, syncparadata, syncfile, HTTPS + at-rest encryption; (D) Server-side data store — PHP 8 + MySQL/MariaDB, command-line relational break-out for dynamic reporting, Process Cases Options to narrow the table set; (E) Monitoring — real-time dashboards, Sync Reports, Map Reports, automated alerts; (F) Operations & exports — Data Viewer download, dataset/case/batch export, sync.log. Preserved the original Operational Role and Project Relevance framing, added an explicit note that F2 has no CSWeb so its operational backplane has to be designed separately. Source_count 1 → 3.
- **Ingest (CSWeb User's Guide)**: Pulled the canonical CSWeb help docs from csprousers.org/help/CSWeb (the documentation that CSPro 8.0 Complete User's Guide repeatedly punts to but doesn't include). Saved 7 HTML pages verbatim to `raw/Documentations/CSWeb-help/`: introduction_to_csweb, adding_roles, adding_users, accessing_data, sync_report, map_report, data_settings. Created `Source - CSWeb Users Guide.md` summarizing the **verified permission model**: only TWO permission axes (5 dashboard checkboxes — data/report/apps/users/roles — and per-dictionary up/download), only TWO built-in roles (Administrator with everything, Standard User with no dashboards but full sync; default fallback for deleted custom roles), custom roles combining any subset of the two axes (≥1 perm required, name immutable). Documented user fields and bulk-CSV format. **Critical correction to the wiki**: the CSWeb User's Guide does NOT document an automated-alerts feature; the inception report's language about "alerts for missing data, upload delays, duplicate entries, non-response patterns, unusual interview duration" is unverifiable against documented CSWeb capabilities — flagged as not-in-the-feature-surface in the concept page. Updated `wiki/concepts/CSWeb.md` (source_count 3→4): removed the unverified alerts claim, added the verified permission model with explicit "what CSWeb does NOT support" callout (no row-level filtering, no time-window perms, no alert-config perms, no "limited dataset"), added a project-wide role mapping that collapses the IR's 10 organizational titles onto 3-4 distinct CSWeb permission shapes (Full admin / Data observer / Operator / Sync identity). Updated `index.md` Sources to add the new CSWeb User's Guide source. Brainstorming context: this ingest was triggered by the F2 PWA admin portal design — Carl asked for 1:1 mirror of CSWeb roles, which forced a verification pass against the actual documentation rather than the project's distilled wiki page.

- **Deliverable + prod fix (CSWeb Sync Report)**: Root-caused and fixed the empty Sync Report tab on `csweb.asiansocial.org` (banner "To view report add a configuration and process the cases"). Three layers: (1) no Data Settings configuration existed (`cspro_dictionaries_schema` empty); (2) no `csweb:process-cases` cron; (3) two CSWeb 8.0.1 bugs hit on F1 — the schema generator maps every alpha item to LONGTEXT so `D_YAKAP_KONSULTA`'s 198 alpha items blew InnoDB's row-size limit (error 1118), and `createDictionarySchema()` runs all DDL as one concatenated PDO prepare so the failure was silent (schema half-created, then stamped valid; F1 breakout job stuck at status=1 with "table d_yakap_konsulta doesn't exist"). Fixed on the box: created `csweb_f1/f3/f4_breakout` DBs + grants, inserted the 3 data-settings rows (mirroring `DataSettings::addDataSetting`, `AES_ENCRYPT(pw,'cspro')`), patched `MySQLDictionarySchemaGenerator.php` (alpha ≤255 → `VARCHAR(len)`) and `DictionarySchemaHelper.php` (per-statement DDL execution, loud failures) with `.orig-8.0.1` backups, regenerated all 3 breakout schemas, reprocessed all 4 test cases (jobs status=2), installed a 5-min flock-guarded cron + logrotate, and verified `/csweb/api/sync-report` returns rows for F1/F3/F4 (OAuth, UI-equivalent headers). Upstream csprousers/csweb main still has both bugs — re-apply patches after any CSWeb upgrade. Full record: `deliverables/CSWeb/CSWeb-Sync-Report-and-Case-Breakout-Setup.md`.

F1 Sync Report now shows facility names (verified: `10280001001` → "BANGUI DISTRICT HOSPITAL"); F3/F4 COALESCE back to raw codes. Three blockers fixed en route: (1) MySQL 8 `ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY` 500s the labeled report query — removed from `sql_mode` via `SET PERSIST` (Carl-approved, matches install-day `log_bin_trust_function_creators` precedent); (2) stock 8.0.1 bug — `importAreaNames()` return-type mismatch (declares CSProResponse, returns StreamedResponse) made every successful import 500 → patch #5 on `api/ReportController.php` (`.orig-8.0.1` backup); (3) discovered label import REPLACES the whole table — always re-upload the full combined CSV. - **F1 CAPI capture — started on emulator (emulator-5554, adb)**: F1 **runs clean** — opens, accepts the 12-digit questionnaire number `010280001001`, advances to Field Control with NO hang and NO SIGSEGV (the two memory-flagged Android blockers are not occurring on this build). Capture pipeline working: `adb exec-out screencap` (adb at `$LOCALAPPDATA/Android/Sdk/platform-tools/`; NOT on PATH; `/sdcard` paths get MSYS-mangled so use exec-out→stdout). Captured + cropped (PIL, keyboard removed) the first 2 real screens — **Questionnaire Number** and **Field Control** — wired into the live crosswalk replacing placeholders (`docs/img/f1-capi-questionnaire-number.png`, `f1-capi-field-control.png`). **Key finding for the crosswalk:** the redesigned form is a combined layout — a left section-navigation tree (Case Key · Field Control · Geographic ID · Facility GPS · A. Facility Head Profile) + the question on the right; advances field-by-field (Enter does NOT reliably advance text fields). **Constraint:** hand-driving all 131 questions interactively (view each screen) is impractical/token-heavy → the scalable path is an **automated adb driver** that walks a pre-scripted valid answer sequence and screencaps each step, run unattended (reused for F3/F4). Proposed as next step; needs the emulator to stay up.
- **F1 CAPI auto-capture driver — v2 built, partial**: Driver (`tmp/f1_capture.py`, adb + uiautomator) walks the form, screencaps each question BEFORE answering (clean slot shots), enters typed values, advances via `questionnaire_fragment_next_button`. **Works for simple fields; stalls at validation chokepoints.** Field Control's **TOTAL_NUMBER_OF_VISITS range-check** loops the driver (dialog masks the questionLabel → handler misses → taps dialog OK repeatedly). Tree-jump can't bypass (CSEntry enforces valid forward completion). Banked: 2 real screens live in crosswalk (QN + Field Control); F1 confirmed running clean (no hang/SIGSEGV). **Assessment:** full-autonomy capture of all 131 Qs needs per-validation hardening (visits range, GPS capture, photo, section skip-logic, exclusivity/other-specify warnings) — a real multi-iteration build, token-heavy to debug live. Pragmatic alt = supervised capture (driver does section selects = the bulk; I clear the few front-matter chokepoints). Decision pending from Carl on effort level.
- **F1 CAPI capture — hit a hard blocker, paused**: Agent-driven adb capture (autonomous driver AND me hand-driving) stalls at the Field Control `TOTAL_NUMBER_OF_VISITS` range-validation — the input renders below the fold and can't be reliably focused/set via adb, so it loops "out of range" and blocks the whole interview. Conclusion: full 131-screen capture by agent-driven adb is not cost-effective in this context (each screen = multiple dump/screencap/verify round-trips); Carl declined to drive manually himself. **Banked:** F1 confirmed runs clean (no hang/SIGSEGV); 2 real CAPI screens live in crosswalk (QN + Field Control); driver (`tmp/f1_capture.py`) + time-lapse watcher (`tmp/watch_capture.py`) scripts built; full F1 paper side staged. **Decision pending from Carl** on path: (A) keep hardening autonomous driver, (B) screen-recording of one interview → frame-extract (fastest, but needs someone to drive once), (C) ship strong crosswalk = real paper pages + real key CAPI screens + per-question 'what differs' notes, fill more screens opportunistically.

The July decks (66-slide FS-training + 24-slide CAPI overview) were **discarded**, not revised — they predate Myra's programmes and were built for a different brief. **Benchmarked first** (DHS ~4-week training w/ a 9-module Master Trainer package + QuizApp; MICS6 CSPro-on-tablets w/ 2 days field practice + pilot and a supervisor-only module; LSMS's progressive ladder group → role-play → classroom-respondent → field) — the common thread is **do-along, not lecture**, which drove the format: every teaching slide is a tablet screenshot on the left and numbered "YOU DO" steps + a CHECK line on the right. **Architecture B (Carl's pick): one module library, two decks.** `modules.js` holds 12 modules A–L authored once, plus `K_FE` (an enumerator escalation variant that stops at the Field Supervisor); `theme.js` holds the Verde Executive look and four layout helpers; the two builders only *compose*, mapping modules onto the real timetable slots. **Field Supervisors deck — 65 slides / 16 images**, Day 2 Los Baños: Module 6 "Using CAPI" (08:00) → F1 + the full case lifecycle (09:00) → F2 (10:30) → Patients (13:00) → **Household (14:30)** → a Day-3 **monitoring handoff pack** (Module L) for the sessions the Project Team runs. **Field Enumerators deck — 62 slides / 14 images**, framed as Module 9 "CAPI Installation", F1/F2/Patients on Day 2 and **Household on Day 3**, no Module L, and an **extra drill slide** on F1/Patients/Household because those slots run 2–3× longer (suspend-and-resume, deliberate hard warning, DK/Refused, opposite patient type, absent household members). **F4 Household is covered in both** — the gap the old decks left open. **Screenshots: 70 real captures, no mockups** — 24 curated from `deliverables/CAPI-Manual/img/` + 46 extracted from `DOH_CAPI_Manual_July27.docx`; all 16 image slides audited against their module's instrument. **Verification: every slide rendered to PNG** (LibreOffice Portable → PDF → PyMuPDF) and eyeballed, then 14/14 automated composition checks. That caught three real defects the build itself reported as fine: (1) pptxgenjs **`LAYOUT_16x9` is 10×5.625in, not 13.333×7.5** — titles and steps were running off the right edge (→ `LAYOUT_WIDE`); (2) **wrong instrument on screen** — the F4 consent slide and the F1 photo/GPS slide were showing **PatientSurvey** captures (F1 case-key re-pointed to a genuine FacilityHeadSurvey shot; F4 consent and F1 GPS demoted to honest bullet slides; the GPS capture re-homed to Module D under a generic eyebrow); (3) the spec's claim that `image43` was the Patient-Type gate was **wrong** — it is Final Result Codes. **Shipped to** `deliverables/training/2026-08-capi-decks/` — both .pptx plus the full build source (`theme.js`, `modules.js`, `shots.js`, both builders, `render_qa.py`, README) **and the 70 screenshots in `shots/`**, with `shots.js` rewritten to relative paths so the decks rebuild from that folder alone (verified by a clean rebuild from that folder alone: 65 slides/16 images and 62/14 reproduced exactly, every embedded image byte-identical to the shipped file). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-29-capi-training-decks-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-29-capi-training-decks.md`. **Open items carried:** (a) **no genuine HouseholdSurvey screenshot exists** in either asset source — F4 consent + photo/GPS stay bullet slides marked "demonstrate live" until real F4 tablet screens are captured; (b) **F2 monitoring discrepancy** — the manual says CSWeb, the live system has a separate HCW console (needs reconciling with Myra before the deck is taught); (c) the FE programme's **13:00 F2 slot has no named in-charge**; (d) **Module B teaches `capi.asiansocial.org/csweb/api`**, so the sync-URL cutover must land before August training; (e) the four-venue FE concurrency conflict (one named CAPI trainer, four simultaneous sites) is still an ASPSI scheduling decision. Not committed (Carl's git).

Counted across the generators: **F1 12, F3 26, F4 26 = 64 exclusive-option questions, all soft** — the pretest touched only F4 and reproduced it on 22 of its 26, so F1/F3 will raise the same complaint the moment anyone tests them. Also labelled outright bugs: **F4 Q141** other-specify prompt fires when the option was **not** ticked ("CAPI error bug") — which **corroborates open #996 from an independent reviewer** (XJ reported the opposite symptom, field *missing*; together they point at one mis-keyed gate; #615 `(Urgent) F4_Section M_Q141` and #667 were closed in June) — and the end-of-interview **date format** rendering `YYYYMMDD` where `MMDDYYYY` is wanted.
