## 2026-08-08 — CAPI Admin Portal: Slice A backend built (E9-ADMIN-004/005/006/007)
- Carl's "let's tackle the capi admin now". Built the module backend against the deployed-but-enforcing-nothing Phase 1 provider: migration `002-admin-portal.sql` (row_version / pw_changed_at / request_id / `console_idem` / `console_svc_tokens`, plus `pbkdf2` in the pw_algo ENUM now so F2 federation never needs an ALTER on live identity data mid-rollout), `admin_bootstrap.php` (one envelope, one CSRF+Origin check, one in-process permission gate, replay protection, catch-all writing to `console_audit` because `log_errors` is Off), users/roles/sessions/audit endpoints behind the single `admin.php` front controller, and the ACL rules they need.
- **E9-ADMIN-004 — the cutover blocker — is closed.** `admin/api.php` now reads `console_users`; its four write actions return 501 with a pointer to the new API; `actor()` prefers `X-Auth-User` then a verified console session then the legacy vars (both of which go empty at cutover, which would have made every audit row `unknown`); and `canary_ok()` is deleted — it probed Apache on loopback with a spoofed Host, bypassing the openresty gate, so after cutover it would have answered 200 forever. A canary that cannot die is worse than none.
- Two escalation guards added during the build that the spec did not ask for: only an owner may grant the owner role or edit an owner's roles (otherwise `admin.users`, which ASPSI holds, is quietly the top of the hierarchy rather than a delegated slice of it), and the owner role can never lose `admin.users`/`admin.system` in the matrix. Plus a pre-cutover `gate_notice` on every credential minted, and a drift report (`htpasswd_only` / `console_only` / `blocking`) naming whoever is about to lose access.
- **229 assertions green** — `test_acl.php` 165 (+19 for the admin surface) and a new `test_admin.php` 64. The new suite caught a real defect in unshipped code: PCRE `$` also matches immediately before a trailing newline, so `"carl\n"` passed username validation; fixed with `\z` in three places. Its first assertion is a drift check between `CONSOLE_PERMS` (acl.php) and `ADM_PERM_KEYS` (admin_roles.php) — divergence there yields either a permission no role can be granted or a tick box no path checks, and both look normal on screen.
- Nothing deployed; migration not applied. Slice A's remainder is all ops: 009 nginx header hygiene, 010 break-glass vhost, 011 login throttle + timing floor, 012 audit tamper-resistance. 010 gates the rest — never flip the gate without a tested way back in.

## 2026-08-08 (2nd run) — Slice A DEPLOYED to production; SC-2 (revocation) now passes
- Carl: "deploy it". Shipped E9-ADMIN-004/005/006/007 to capi.asiansocial.org 14:08–14:20 UTC. Migration 002 applied and then re-applied to prove idempotency (8 tables all InnoDB, `pbkdf2` in the ENUM, `request_id` + both indexes, 18/18 `pw_changed_at` backfilled). **232 assertions green in the container on PHP 8.1** (test_acl 165, test_admin 67) — the box runs 8.1, dev runs 8.3, so this was worth re-running rather than assuming.
- **No container restart**, because nothing in the deploy adds an env var — so MySQL never bounced and CSEntry sync held 200 throughout. The gate is untouched: `/docs/dashboard.html` still 401s via Apache exactly as before. Pre-overwrite copies at `/opt/app/lamp/private/_pre-e9admin-20260808-140810/`.
- **E2E against the live front door** with a disposable `zzt_owner` (removed afterwards; `verify` = TOTAL LEFTOVER=0). The proof that matters: a live session answering 200, disabled by an owner, then **401 on the same cookie with no restart**. That is SC-2, which scored 0 this morning. Also verified: CSRF-missing 403 · duplicate 409 · idempotent password reset returns the *identical* temp password on replay · full-set role PUT replays as `changed:false` · stale `row_version` 409 · self-disable 409 · `must_change` blocks the admin API until changed. All three escalation guards held — a `programme_admin` was refused on granting `owner`, on editing an owner's roles, and on editing role permissions.
- Legacy screen verified in-process (it sits behind htpasswd, so it can't be driven over HTTP without an operator credential): `?r=users` reads console_users, all four writes return the 501 `moved` envelope, and **`.htpasswd-docs` plus all three `.htaccess` gate files have unchanged mtimes** — the 501 paths wrote nothing.
- **Drift report: `htpasswd_only: []`.** Every account that can reach the console today exists in console_users with a role. Nothing loses access when the gate flips.
- Two things found in the run: `cplreyes` (the sole owner) still has `must_change=1` like all 18 imported accounts, so at cutover he is forced through a password change on first sign-in — fine, but not a surprise you want in a quiet window. And create *lowercases* a username before validating, so `ZZT_Bad` was stored as `zzt_bad`; the behaviour is right (login matches case-insensitively anyway) but the code comment claimed rejection — comment corrected and the real contract pinned with 3 tests.

## 2026-08-09 — CAPI Admin Portal UI shipped (Slice B, E9-ADMIN-020…028)
- Carl: "create the CAPI Admin Portal ui ux, align it with CAPI console". Built six screens on the console's own design system rather than a second visual language: `portal.css` ("statistical instrument panel" — institutional serif headings, verde brand, graph-paper canvas, tabular-mono figures) stays untouched and `admin.css` only adds. Files: `ui.js` (DOM kit, both API clients, hash router, destructive-action dialog), `admin.css`, `view-{users,roles,sessions,audit,account}.js`, `boot.js`, rewritten `index.php`, refactored `app.js`. Deployed 17:02 UTC; Activities/Alerting/Plan logic left verbatim since they are live during pretest.
- **Rendered and iterated before shipping**, not asserted: a throwaway Playwright harness served the real CSS/JS against fixtures shaped like the deployed API. That caught five real defects — (1) native `<dialog>` pinned to the top-left because portal.css opens with `* { margin: 0 }`, which zeroes the `margin:auto` that centres a modal; (2) filter labels rendering as inline body text because `.adm-bar label` was never styled, only `.adm-f label`; (3) role-matrix headers reading verb-first ("VIEW / MONITORING", and two unrelated columns both titled "ADMIN"); (4) four-line-tall user rows that buried the duplicate-spelling warning; (5) a right-aligned numeric column dragging its generated label right in the sub-640px card layout.
- **Damage prevention is the point of the Users screen.** `confirmDestructive()` opens in a checking state, *fetches* the blast radius, keeps the button disabled until it resolves, requires the exact username typed, and blocks permanently if the preflight fails — a dialog that cannot say what it will do does not get to do it. No optimistic UI anywhere: every mutation is followed by a re-read. Verified rendering "Signs out 1 live session immediately."
- **Accessibility measured, not assumed.** Recomputed `--ink-3` #74838c on white = 3.91:1 (fails AA) and the replacement #5c6a73 = 5.58:1 before using either. Shipped focus-visible rings (portal.css gives buttons none), skip link, aria-live, aria-current, focus-to-main on route change, reduced-motion, 44px targets, and a `data-label` card layout below 640px that `tbl()` backfills automatically so a call site cannot forget it.
- Also closed a hole the new Audit screen would have had: `admin/api.php`'s `audit()` now writes to **both** the file trail and `console_audit`, so alerting/plan/activity changes appear there instead of being silently absent from something calling itself the audit trail.
- Post-deploy: every new asset 401s to anonymous, gate unchanged, CSEntry sync 200. `index.php` rendered in-container resolves identity from `X-Auth-User` (the cutover-safe path) and emits all 8 scripts and routes. **E9-ADMIN-026 is partial** — the SPA nav is `/me`-driven, but `build_portal.py` and `portal_shell.py` still carry duplicate nav code for the generated pages.

## 2026-08-09 — /goal complete the CAPI admin portal: Slices A+B closed, 29/34 (E9-ADMIN-009…045)
- Eleven backlog items in one pass. **Slice A and Slice B are now complete**; the module stands at 29 of 34, ~14 h left, and what remains is three things of which two are Carl's call.
- **A live defect found and fixed first.** E9-ADMIN-004 made `actor()` trust `X-Auth-User` (it must — the legacy vars go empty at cutover), but the vhost never blanked the client's copy. Blanked in `/docs/` and `/`, re-verified `(absent)`, reflector removed. `/csweb/` untouched.
- **Break-glass (010) built as a loopback listener, not a public alt port** — this host has NO OS firewall (iptables ACCEPT, no ufw), so an open port is world-reachable. `127.0.0.1:8443` TLS + basic auth over `ssh -L`, with a *frozen* copy of `.htpasswd-docs` so it depends on nothing the cutover changes. Proven 401→200→PHP with a disposable credential. Also killed the plan's IP-allowlist idea: Carl's address changed overnight (`.46.155`→`.45.159`), visible in the audit trail.
- **011** per-IP throttle before the user lookup + a 250 ms response floor; the dummy argon2id hash is gone (it inverted the oracle — real apr1 accounts answered *faster* than fictional ones). Measured from Manila: 468/459/456/477 ms across nonexistent vs three real accounts — ordering now inside the noise. **012** `console_audit` is `SELECT,INSERT` only; DELETE and UPDATE denied to the app, all six needed DML paths still work.
- **030** one query for session+roles+perms+version (`GROUP_CONCAT DISTINCT` + a correlated subquery — a plain SUM over that join double-counts): **~5 statements per request → 2.45, measured**, write now conditional. Re-proved invalidation after the rewrite (200 → version bump → 401). nginx `proxy_cache` deliberately left off and documented: it would trade a 10-second revocation SLA for the last round trip.
- **031** xdebug off — `xdebug.mode = off` **unquoted parses as boolean false** and yielded `''`; caught by reading the value back, fixed with quotes. Same restart turned `log_errors` on. 1.9 s downtime, database untouched. **032** `whoami.php` reshimmed — it is the `/_capi_auth` target for `location /`, so left alone it would have 401'd the entire static portal into a redirect loop at cutover.
- **040** `test_lib` + `test_db`: **332 assertions total** across four suites. apr1 cross-checked against six `openssl passwd -apr1` vectors (a different implementation), including short salt, empty password and multi-byte ñ. The DB suite immediately found a real inconsistency: `revoke_user` counted already-dead rows while the dialog promises "signs out N *live* sessions" — now revokes all, counts live.
- **041** `cutover-check.sh`, 25 checks, canary-vs-non-canary exit codes. **043** nightly GC + `RETENTION.md`. **044** `DATA-SUBJECT-RIGHTS.md` against verified schema, with erasure explicitly gated on ASPSI/DOH sign-off. **045** `__Host-` cookies, CSP with a nonce on the sign-in page, `Cache-Control: no-store` at the nginx layer.
- **Found while verifying NFR-04: the estate's nightly SQL dump had never worked.** `preBackup.sh` assigned the root password to a variable then ran `mysqldump --password=` empty; exit 1045 every night, and `postBackup.sh` deleted the near-empty file so it left no trace. **Every borg snapshot contained an empty dump — csweb_uhc_y2, csweb_f2, all breakouts, not just capi_auth.** One-line fix; dump is now ~18 MB across all eight databases.
- **Cutover staged, not executed.** `ops/cutover.sh` + a post-cutover vhost that passes `openresty -t`; preflight, backup, apply, re-check, **automatic rollback on canary failure**; `--dry-run` and `--rollback` both exercised. Not flipped because all 18 accounts still have `must_change=1`: the flip forces every user through a password change, unannounced, mid-pretest — and while the config rollback is clean, the human one is not (a password changed after the flip is dead after a rollback). That is a scheduling and comms decision.
- Final state: 25/25 checks green, 332 assertions green, zero test residue, CSEntry sync 200 throughout.

## 2026-08-09 — CUTOVER DONE. authz.php is the gate; Slices A, B and C complete (32/34)

- **Two gaps caught in the final preflight, before the flip — both would have broken things immediately.** (1) Deny-by-default would have 403'd the static portal's own assets: `/portal.css`, `/assets/`, `/platform/`, `/uhc/`, `/about/`, `/index.html` had no ACL rule, so capi-www would have rendered with no stylesheet — an outage to the eye. All three are now stripped, backed up, and restored by `--rollback`.

Both matrices correct: field_supervisor 403s the data room, client_viewer 403s respondent detail, analyst 403s admin, an unlisted path 403s for everyone, anonymous 302s to sign-in. SC-6 partial: break-glass proven end to end; `--rollback` written and dry-run but deliberately not executed. **SC-4 still fails** — no MFA and all 18 accounts still carry `must_change=1`.
- Gotcha for future probes: `test_db.php`'s cleanup predicate is `zzt\_%` and swept my `zzt_r_*` role fixtures mid-check. Harmless in production (no real account starts with `zzt_`) but probe accounts need a different prefix — used `zzq_r_*`.

## 2026-08-11 (LATER-2) — #1190 logo covers DEPLOYED: F1 v1.3.4 / F3 v1.4.3 / F4 v1.5.11, all byte-verified
- Carl: "deploy" → "publish and deploy". Bumped all three (stamp_version bump = pff restamp + qsf regen with the logo data-URI ×8 languages), verify_questions PASS ×3, Designer compile clean ×3, deployed to CSWeb, byte-verified each published pen (logo + version + SSRCS present). GH #1190 commented (stays open for ASPSI's title wording); patch notes posted to #f1/#f3/#f4-uat with remove+re-add.
- **INCIDENT (F4) — publish misfire, editor corruption, full recovery.** The `csweb_deploy_designer.py click` for F4's Publish-and-Deploy never landed: the driver's ABSOLUTE screen click needs the Designer foreground, and a restored CSDeploy dialog had stolen it. Stray keystrokes leaked into the logic editor ("lyPROC…" — a selection got REPLACED), and `auto_deploy` then locked onto a **stale parked CSDeploy** (proc start 07:56 < the 09:33 qsf bump) because package-name matching can't tell twins apart. The save prompt later confirmed the damage: answering Yes (hash-guarded) wrote an apc missing PROC BARANGAY + PROC LATITUDE in a 3,308-line reserialization. Recovery: killed the poisoned dialog + Designer, restored the generator apc from the worktree (md5 4b86820d), fresh compile, republished via **WM_COMMAND 44038 posted straight to the Designer hwnd** (no focus needed), fresh CSDeploy confirmed by process start time, deployed, verified.
- **TRAP — pen byte-verify MUST be alignment-independent.** `decompressed.decode('utf-16-le')` over the whole blob silently misses any text section at an ODD byte offset — it produced false "stale pen" negatives that drove half this incident (F4's logo was at odd offset 1856803 all along). The correct check: `bytes.find(needle.encode('utf-16-le'))` on the bz2-decompressed pen. Corollary: the 10:04 "stale" deploy was probably content-correct (CSDeploy packages disk state at Deploy-click), and the suspicion that F4 v1.5.10 shipped without its SSRCS line is WITHDRAWN — same false negative.
- **TRAP — Designer save-prompt semantics**: "No" on "Application …ent has changed. Save?" ABORTS Publish-and-Deploy (CSPro insists on saving before packaging); it does not publish-from-disk. If memory state is suspect: hash every source file, answer Yes, diff hashes — the one changed file tells the truth.
- **New robust publish path** (message-based, zero focus): File menu = submenu 1 of `GetMenu(designer)`; **Publish and Deploy = WM_COMMAND 44038** (Publish .pen = 44037, Edit-Undo = 57643; MRU anchor 57616 confirms the map). PostMessage + BM_CLICK on any prompt + fresh-CSDeploy-by-start-time = deploy without touching the foreground. Also: every Publish-and-Deploy spawns its OWN CSDeploy.exe process — a "fresh publish" that adds no new process DID NOT HAPPEN; that process-list check is the cheap gate that caught everything.
- Deploy-dialog census after this session: fresh parked trio F1 hwnd 2165790 (09:36) / F3 1639956 (09:39) / F4 2624734 (10:30); three OLD ones from the morning wave remain (pids 4064/7556/31488) — duplicates make auto_deploy's package-name match ambiguous; recommend Carl close the old three. Worktree mirrored with deployed versions.json + qsf/pff ×3; deliverables left uncommitted for Carl per standing rule.

- **EMULATOR TRAP — cold-boot sideload perms**: after a cold AVD boot, adb-created folders under csentry/ come up `shell:ext_data_rw` with NO group-write (`drwxr-s---`) → CSEntry fails "Could not open the data source …: Could not create a new data file" on case creation. Fix: `adb root` then chown -R u0_a192:ext_data_rw + chmod -R 770 on the app folder at /data/media/0/... (the FUSE path masks the real perms). Also: first push right after boot can fail "remote secure_mkdirs failed" — wait ~10 s for storage to settle and re-push.
- #1190 remaining: title reference-year wording only.
